(nālintiṇi) 11. muppāl (tirukuṟaḷ). 12. tirikaṭukam. 13. ācārak kōvi. 14. paḻamoḻi nāṉūṟu. 15. ciṟu pañcamūlam. 16. mutumoḻik kāñci. 17. ēlāti 18. kinnili.
kinnili
intap patiṉeṭṭuk kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷum ippōtu namakkuk kiṭittirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kinnili eṉṉum nūl palarukkut teriyāmalum acciṟ patippikkappaṭāmalum iruntatu. akkālattil cilar “iṉṉili” eṉpatu patiṉeṭṭu nūlkaḷil oṉṟu eṉṟu karutiṉārkaḷ. 1888-ām āṇṭil tiru. ta. kaṉakacuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ, tamakkuk kiṭitta mikap paḻiya ēṭṭuccuvaṭi yoṉṟil kinnili eṉpatu kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷil oṉṟu eṉṟu eḻutiyiruntatik kaṇṭu ataṉit tāmōtaram piḷḷi yavarkaḷukkut terivittārkaḷ. piṟaku, kinnili kīḻkkaṇakku nūlic cērntatu eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, anta nūliṉ pirati avarkaḷukkuk kiṭikkavilli.
tiru. ti. celvakēcava mutaliyāravarkaḷ kinnili eṉṉum nūl oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉpati nampavilli. avar 1893-ām āṇṭil acciṭṭa ācārak kōvi eṉṉum kīḻkkaṇakku nūliṉ patippuriyil itupaṟṟi eḻutiyuḷḷār. “ituvariyil kiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷa ilakkiya ilakkaṇa urikaḷilum kinnilic ceyyuḷ mēṟkōḷkaḷāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷatākavum evarkkum kaṭpulaṉāyiṟ ṟilli. ākavē, kinniliyik kiyil nilipeṟak kāṇuntaṉiyum uṟuti kūṟutalākātu” eṉṟu avar eḻutiyuḷḷār.
kinnili eṉṉum kīḻkkaṇakku nūliṉ ēṭṭup pirati 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutivariyilum kiṭikkavilli. ākavē, acciṭappaṭavilli. piṟaku, inta 20-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē 1931-ām āṇṭilē kinnili ēṭṭup pirati kiṭittu acciṭappaṭṭatu. ataṉ ēṭṭuppiratiyip peṟṟu acciṭṭavar tiru. ananta rāmiyaravarkaḷ. itu veḷivanta piṟaku, kīḻkkaṇakkic cērnta paḻiyanūl itu eṉpatu uṟutiyāyiṟṟu. piṉṉar 1936-ām āṇṭil va.u. citamparampiḷḷiyavarkaḷum, es. viyāpurip piḷḷi yavarkaḷum cērntu patippitta tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram iḷampūraṇar uriyilē kinnilic ceyyuṭkaḷ nāṉku iṭaṅkaḷil mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatik kāṇamuṭintatu. eṉavē, kinnili patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlic cērntatu eṉpatum, ataṉ ceyyuṭkaḷi iḷampūraṇa aṭikaḷ eṉṉum uriyāciriyarum tamatu uriyil mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭiyuḷḷār eṉpatum teriyalāyiṉa. kinnili akapporuḷ ilakkiya nūl.
iṉṉili
ippeyariyuṭiya nūl oṉṟu aṇmikkālattil veḷivantuḷḷatu. itu kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ patiṉeṭṭil oṉṟu eṉṟu tavaṟākak karutappaṭṭatu. piṟaku, itu kīḻkkaṇakkic cērnta nūlaṉṟu eṉṟum pōli nūleṉṟum aṟiyappaṭṭatu. iṉṟum cilar itaṉik kīḻkkaṇakku nūl eṉṟu uṇmiyaṟiyāmal nampukiṉṟārkaḷ. ākavē. innūlip paṟṟiya varalāṟṟik kūṟa vēṇṭuvatu muṟiyākum.
kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷik kūṟukiṟa ‘nālaṭi nāṉmaṇi’ eṉattoṭaṅkum paḻiya veṇpāviṉ piṉ iraṇṭaṭikaḷ,
	“iṉṉili col kāñciyuṭaṉ ēlāti eṉpavē
 kinniliya vāṅkīḻk kaṇakku”
eṉpatu. itil kāñcikku (mutumoḻik kāñcikku) aṭimoḻiyākiya iṉṉili col eṉpati iṉṉili, iṉcol eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa iraṇṭu nūlkaḷ eṉṟu ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta ci.vi. tāmōtarampiḷḷiyavarkaḷ karutiṉārkaḷ. kinnili eṉpatu oru nūliṉ peyar eṉpati avar aṟiya villi.
piṉṉar, kinnili eṉpatu kīḻkkaṇakkil oru nūl eṉpatik kaṉakacuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷāl aṟinta piṟaku 1889-ām āṇṭil, “iṉṉili col eṉpataṟku iṉṉili, iṉcol eṉa iraṇṭu nūlkaḷākātu kāñcikku vicēṣaṇamākavum, kinnili yeṉṟatu vēṟoru taṉi nūlākavum koḷḷattakum” eṉṟu ilakkaṇa viḷakkap patippuriyilē eḻutiṉārkaḷ. āṉālum iṉṉili eṉpatu oru nūliṉ peyar eṉṟu vēṟu cilar akkālattil nampiṉārkaḷ. nampiṉatu maṭṭumallāmal annūlip peṟat tēṭiṉārkaḷ. ivvāṟu patiṉeṇ kīḻk kaṇakku nūlkaḷil patiṉēḻu nūlkaḷ iṉṉavi eṉṟu aiyamillāmal uṟutiyākat terinta piṟaku oru nūlippaṟṟi maṭṭum karuttu vēṟṟumi iruntatu. atu, patiṉeṭṭāvatu nūl kinnili eṉṟu cilarum iṉṉili eṉṟum vēṟu cilarum karutiyatākum. ivvāṟu irucārārāl karutappaṭṭa iraṇṭu nūlkaḷum 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kiṭikka villi.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilē iraṇṭu nūlkaḷiṉ ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷum kiṭittaṉa. iṉṉili ēṭṭuccuvaṭi 1915-ām āṇṭilum kinnili ēṭṭuccuvaṭi 1931-ām āṇṭilum acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭaṉa. (kinniliyip paṟṟi mēlē eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ) iṉṉili ēṭṭuccuvaṭiyik kaṇṭu piṭittavar vittuvāṉ ta. mu. corṇampiḷḷi yavarkaḷ. intac cuvaṭiyit tiru. va.u.  citamparampiḷḷi yavarkaḷ 1915-ām āṇṭil acciṟ patippittārkaḷ.
innūl aṟappāl, poruṭpāl, iṉpappāl, vīṭṭuppāl eṉṟu nāṉku pirivuṭiyatu. nāṟpattintu veṇpākkaḷi yuṭiyatu. “poykiyār iṉṉili” eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭap paṭṭirukkiṟatu. itil periya viyappu eṉṉaveṉṟāl, pāratam pāṭiya peruntēvaṉār innūlukkuk kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭiyiruppatutāṉ!
innūl mukavuriyil, va.u. citamparampiḷḷiyavarkaḷ innūl ēṭṭup piratiyip paṟṟi ivvāṟu eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
“eṉakkuk kiṭitta innūliṉ ēṭṭuppirati tirunelvēli jillā āḻvār tirunakariyil mūṉṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku  muṉṉar avatarittut tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi muṟṟuṟak kaṟṟu ‘māṟaṉalaṅkāram’ mutaliya cila ariya nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟiyaruḷiya srīmāṉ irattiṉak kavirāyaravarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu... ivvēṭṭup piratiyi eṉakku aḷittavarkaḷ tirunelvēli hintukkallūrit tamiḻp paṇṭitar srīmāṉ ta. mu. corṇam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷukku ivvēṭṭup piratiyi vēṟupala toṉṉūlēṭṭuppiratikaḷōṭu cērttaḷittavarkaḷ tirumēṉik kavirāyaravarkaḷ cantatiyārkaḷukkut talivarākavum, eṉakku uṟaviṉarākavum naṇparākavum, tirunelvēli jillā tālukkā pōrṭṭu memparākavum viḷaṅkum srī a. mī. maliyiyāp piḷḷiyavarkaḷ.  itukāṟum innūliṉ peyarum, innūl kīḻkkaṇakkup patiṉeṭṭil oṉṟeṉpatum teriyātirunta nam tamiḻ nāṭṭārkku innūli aḷippataṟkuk kāraṇa karttarkaḷāyirunta ivviruvarkaḷukkum tamiḻulakam naṉṟi pārāṭṭakkaṭaṉ paṭṭuḷḷatu.”
iṉṉili nūlāciriyarip paṟṟik kūṟukiṟa iṭattil patippāciriyarum uriyāciriyarumākiya va.u. citamparam piḷḷi yavarkaḷ ivvāṟu eḻutukiṟār. “innūlit tokuttavar maturi yāciriyar eṉṟu innūliṉ ēṭṭup piratiyiṉ mutal ēṭṭil eḻutap paṭṭirukkiṟateṉṟu mēlē kūṟiyuḷḷēṉ. aḵtāvatu, innūlik kīḻkkaṇakkup patiṉeṭṭil oṉṟākat tokuttavar maturi yāciriyar eṉṟu koḷka. ‘maturiyāciriyar’ eṉṉum ciṟappup peyarip peṟṟu viḷaṅkiyavar māṟaṉār eṉpavarum nallantuvaṉār eṉpavarumē. ivviruvarum kaṭic caṅkap pulavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷil evar innūlit tokuttavareṉpatu teriyavilli. innūṟkuk kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭiyavar pāratampāṭiya peruntēvar. ivar eṭṭut toki nūlkaḷil cilavaṟṟiṟkuk kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭiyatupōla innūṟkum pāṭiyuḷḷār. ivarum kaṭiccaṅkap pulavar”
ivar kūṟiyuḷḷati varalāṟṟōṭu oppiṭṭu ārāyntu pārttāl, iṉṉili nūl orupōli nūl eṉpatum, atu kīḻkkaṇakku nūlic cērntatu aṉṟu eṉpatum paṭṭappakal pōl viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kīḻkkaṇakku nūlip patiṉeṭṭākat tokuttavaryār eṉpatu oruvarukkum teriyātu. atupaṟṟik karṇaparampariyākavō nūlmūlamākavō yātoru ceytiyum kiṭiyātu. āṉāl, kaṭiccaṅkap pulavarāṉa maturiyāciriyar kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷit tokuttatāka iṉṉilip patippāciriyar kūṟukiṟār. kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ patiṉeṭṭum kaṭiccaṅka kālattukku muṉpu iyaṟṟap paṭṭavi alla. ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭu toṭaṅki ki.pi. 8-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil vevvēṟu kālattil vevvēṟu pulavarkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭavi kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ. nālaṭi nāṉūṟu ki.pi. 8-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. muppāl eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷ ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil allatu ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ ki.pi. 8-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṟaku tokukkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum  kaṭiccaṅka kālattil 
(ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil) tokukkappaṭṭiruttal muṭiyātu. āṉāl, iṉṉili patippāciriyar, kaṭiccaṅkap pulavarāṉa maturi yāciriyar kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷit tokuttatāka iṉṉili ēṭṭuccuvaṭi kūṟukiṟateṉṟu eḻutukiṟār. itaṉip pakuttaṟivuḷḷār evvāṟu ēṟṟukkoḷḷamuṭiyum?
	“neṭuveṇ pāṭṭē munnāṉkaṭittē
 kuṟuveṇ pāṭṭiṟ kaḷaveḻu cīrē.”
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttira (ceyyuḷiyal 159.) uriyil pērāciriyar eḻutiya viḷakkam iṅkuk karutattakkatu:
“iṅṅaṉam aḷaviyal veṇpāc ciṟappuṭittāta ṉōkkip patiṉeṇ kīḻk kaṇakkiṉuḷḷum muttoḷḷāyirattum āṟaṭiyi ṉēṟāmaṟ ceyyuḷ ceytār piṟcāṉnrumeṉak koḷka.” eṉṟu pērāciriyar eḻutukiṟār. kīḻkkaṇakku nūlāciriyarkaḷip piṟcāṉṟōr eṉṟu avar kūṟuvatu nōkkuka. piṟcāṉṟōr eṉṟāl, kaṭic caṅka kālattiṟkuppiṉ irunta cāṉṟōr eṉpatu poruḷ. eṉavē piṟkālattuc cāṉṟōr iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷi muṟkālattuc cāṉṟōr oruvar tokuttār eṉṟu kūṟuvatu evvāṟu poruntum? eṉavē, iṉṉili ēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyil eḻutappaṭṭatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟa, kīḻk kaṇakku nūlkaḷi maturiyāciriyar eṉṉum kaṭic caṅka kālattup pulavar tokuttār eṉṉum kūṟṟup pōlik kūṟṟāmeṉ ṟaṟika.
aṭuttapaṭiyāka, iṉṉili nūlukkup pāratam pāṭiya peruntēvaṉār kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭiṉār eṉpatu. ituvum pōlik kūṟṟākum. kīḻk kaṇakku nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku antanta nūlāciriyarkaḷē kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar. cila kīḻk kaṇakku nūlkaḷukkuk kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu illi. āṉāl, kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ etaṟkumē, pāratam pāṭiya peruntēvaṉār kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭavilli. iṉṉili nūlukku maṭṭum kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭiṉār eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. iṉṉili, kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ patiṉeṭṭil oṉṟāka iruntāl, itaṟku maṭṭum pāram pāṭiya peruntēvaṉār ēṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭa vēṇṭum? ēṉiya patiṉēḻu nūlkaḷukku ēṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu ivar pāṭavilli. toki nūlkaḷ patiṉeṭṭil patiṉēḻu nūlkaḷukkuk kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭāmal oru nūlukku maṭṭum ēṉ pāṭiṉār? illi. kīḻkkaṇakkut toki nūlkaḷ patiṉeṭṭukkum cērttu ikkaṭavuḷ vāḻttip pāṭiṉār eṉṟāl, ēṉiya kīḻkkaṇakku ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷ ovvoṉṟilum intak kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuc ceyyuḷ irukka vēṇṭum allavā? avikaḷil pāratam pāṭiya peruntēvaṉāriṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuk kāṇappaṭāmal, inta iṉṉili ēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyil maṭṭum, (itu kīḻk kaṇakku nūlic cērntatum aṉṟu.) kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭiṉār eṉṟāl, itaṉip pakuttaṟivuḷḷār eppaṭi oppukkoḷḷamuṭiyum? eṉavē, iṉṉilip patippāciriyar, annūl muṉṉuriyilum, āciriyarip paṟṟik kūṟiya iṭattilum, iṉṉili kīḻk kaṇakku nūlkaḷil oṉṟeṉṟu kūṟiyatu ēṟkattakkataṉṟu.
mēlum iṉṉilip patippāciriyar va.u. citamparam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ, iṉṉili nūl ceyyuṭkaḷi uriyāciriyarākiya iḷampūrāṇa aṭikaḷ tolkāppiya uriyil mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭiyuḷḷār eṉṟum ākavē iṉṉili paḻiya nūl eṉṟum mēṟpaṭi nūliṉ muṉṉuriyil eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. iḷampūraṇar tamatu tolkāppiyak kaḷaviyal 23-ām cūttira uriyilum, kaṟpiyal 5-ām cūttira uriyilum 12-ām cūttiruriyilum, iṉṉili nūṟ ceyyuṭkaḷi mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭiyiruppatāka eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. ivar kūṟukiṟapaṭi icceyyuṭkaḷi iḷampūraṇa aṭikaḷ mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭukiṟārā eṉpatip palamuṟi tēṭit tēṭip pārttēṉ. oriṭattilāvatu mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭappaṭṭa villi.
tiru. es. viyāpurip piḷḷiyavarkaḷum itu paṟṟi itiyē kūṟukiṟār:
“innūṟ (iṉṉili nūṟ) ceyyuṭkaḷi iḷam pūraṇam eṭuttāṇṭatāka aintu iṭaṅkaḷ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi yaṉittum iḷampūraṇaruri ēṭṭuppiratiyilum. accup piratiyilum kāṇappaṭavilli, poruḷatikārattil ceyyuḷiyal tavira ēṉiya iyalkaḷukkut tamiḻnāṭu muḻuvatilum uḷḷa ēṭṭup pirati oṉṟēyām. iraṇṭāvatu oru piratiyilli eṉpatu iṅkē maṉaṅkoḷḷat takkatu.”3
eṉavē, iṉṉili nūṟceyyuṭkaḷi iḷampūraṇa aṭikaḷ tamatu tolkāppiya uriyil mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭiyuḷḷār eṉṟu innūṟ patippāciriyarākiya va.u. citamparam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ. mukavuriyil eḻutiyiruppatu tavaṟāṉatu: uṇmikku māṟāṉatu. itil iṉṉoru viyappu eṉṉaveṉṟāl, tiru va.u.ci. piḷḷiyavarkaḷ. es. viyāpurip piḷḷiyuṭaṉ cērntu tol kāppiyam, poruḷātikāram, iḷampūraṇar uri muḻuvatiyum acciṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. eṉpatutāṉ.”4
iṉṉili nūli acciṭṭu irupattōru āṇṭukkup piṉṉar iḷampūraṇar uriyi acciṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. itaṉ patippuriyil, va.u. citamparam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ, eḻutiyuḷḷa kīḻkkaṇṭa pakuti, iṉṉilic ceyyuṭkaḷi iḷampūraṇar
tamatu uriyil mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭiyuḷḷār eṉṟu iṉṉuri muṉṉuriyil ivar eḻutiyatu tavaṟu eṉṟu kūṟuvatupōla (veḷippaṭiyāka illāmal) kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. tolkāppiyam, poruḷātikāram, iḷampūraṇar urip patippil piḷḷi yavarkaḷ eḻutuvatu itu:
“tamiḻnāṭu muḻuvatilum poruḷatikāra iḷam pūraṇaruri muṟṟumaṭaṅkiya pirati oṉṟēyuḷḷatu. ippōtu aṅkaṅkē oru cilariṭattuḷḷa piratikaḷaṉittum ivvēṭṭup piratiyip pārtteḻutik koṇṭa kaṭitap piratikaḷēyākum. ik kaṭitap piratikaḷ cilavaṟṟil oru cilaviṭaṅkaḷil ēṭṭup piratiyiṟ kāṇppeṟāta viṣayaṅkaḷ ātāramiṉṟi nuḻitteḻutappaṭṭaṉa. avvāṟu nuḻitteḻutiyavaṟṟi yellām kaḷintu ēṭṭup piratiyi luḷḷavāṟē ippatippu eṉatu naṇpar viyāpurip piḷḷiyavar kaḷāṟ cittañ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.”
ivvāṟu 1936-il eḻutiya tiru. va. u. ci. avarkaḷ, tām 1915-il patippitta iṉṉili nūl muṉṉuriyil eḻutiyavi. “ātāramiṉṟi nuḻitteḻutappaṭṭaṉa” eṉpati veḷippaṭi yākak kūṟāmal maṟimukamāka oppuk koḷkiṟār.
iṉi, iṉṉili patippuriyil va.u.ci avarkaḷ, pērāciriyarum, uriyāciriyarum. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum tamatu tolkāppiya uriyilum, yāpparuṅkala viruttiyurikārar tamatu uriyilum iṉṉili nūliliruntu ceyyuṭkaḷi mēṟkoḷ kāṭṭiruppatākak kuṟippiṭṭu, “ivvāṟu tollāciriyarkaḷum tolluri yāciriyarkaḷum innūliṉi eṭuttāṇṭiruppatē innūliṉ mēmpāṭṭik kāṭṭā niṟkum” eṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār. ituvum, “ātāramiṉṟi nuḻitteḻutappaṭṭa” uṇmikku māṟāṉatākum. itupaṟṟiyum tiru. es. viyāpurip piḷḷi yavarkaḷ. kūṟiyiruppatu karutattakkatu.
“pērāciriyarum. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum innūli (iṉṉili  nūli) eṭuttāṇṭatāka srī piḷḷi (va.u.ci.) avarkaḷ kūṟuki ṟārkaḷ. āṉāl, avarkaḷ kāṭṭum iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum pūtattār aviyaṭakku eṉakkāṇappaṭukiṟatēyaṉṟip poykiyārip paṟṟiyēṉum, iṉṉiliyip paṟṟiyēṉum oṉṟum kuṟippiṭappaṭa villi. kaṭiciyāka, yāpparuṅkala viruttiyil oru ceyyuḷ iṉṉiliyiṉ kaṇṇē uḷḷatu eṉap piḷḷi yavarkaḷ kāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ. āṇṭum acceyyuḷ oḷaviyār pāṭiyatākak koḷḷak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.”5
itaṉāl, iṉṉili eṉṉum nūl kīḻk kaṇakku nūlic cērntatu aṉṟu eṉpatum, atu oru pōli nūl eṉpatum terikiṉṟaṉa.
ceṉṉi mākāṇak kallūriyil tamiḻāciriyarāka irunta anantarāma ayyaravarkaḷ. 1931-ām āṇṭu kinniliyi acciṭṭa piṟaku. iṉṉili pōlinūl eṉpatu veḷḷiṭi malipōl viḷaṅkiviṭṭatu.








kampa irāmāyaṇam
(innūl 118-ārampattil kūṟiyuḷḷa ‘kampa irāmāyaṇam’ eṉpataṉ toṭarpuri.)
1886-ām āṇṭil acciṭappaṭṭa ēreḻupatu,  tirukki vaḻakkam eṉṉum puttakattukku mukavuri eḻutiya toḻuvūr vēlāyuta mutaliyār avarkaḷ, akkālattil acciṭappaṭṭa kampa rāmāyaṇattil ataṉi acciṭṭōr camayak kāḻppuk kāraṇamāka eṉṉeṉṉa māṟupāṭukaḷ ceytārkaḷ eṉpati anta mukavuriyil eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. antap pakutiyik kīḻē tarukiṉṟēṉ:
“tēvārattuḷ ‘pattūrpuk kirantuṇṭu’ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavum, tiruvācakattu ‘ēkācamiṭṭa viruṭikaḷ’ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavum, periyapurāṇattuḷ ‘kātil veṇkuḻiyōṉ kaḻaṟoḻa neṭiyōṉ kālam pārttiruntatu maṟiyāṉ’ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavum eṅṅaṉam veḷḷip pāṭalāka vaḻaṅkiyatō, avvaṇṇamē irāmāyaṇat tuḷḷum veḷḷippāṭal vaḻaṅkiṟṟākaliṉ, civapakkamāṉa ceyyuḷu maviyēyeṉpāru muḷarālōveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu.
“tēvāra mutaliyavaṟṟuṭ kalanta avveḷḷip pāṭalic civarkaḷ yāru muṭaṉpaṭṭapaṭi yaṉṟu. yāmu matuvē. atu niṟka, māṇikkamum pōlimaṇiyum, viramum paḷiṅku mutaliya veḷḷik kallum viravac ceytuḻip pārvi vallāṉ piritteṭukkun tiṟam pōlum, cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷum piṉṉi yōr ceyyuḷum, avvāṟē moḻikaḷum mayaṅkiyavaḻic cevivallāṉ pirittuṇarn tuṇarttumaṉṟē........
“allatūum, veḷḷip pāṭṭeṉac cilavaṟṟik kūṟuvār tammāṟ kampar pāṭṭeṉa vuṭaṉpaṭṭa vēṉiya ceyyuḷōṭi yāvaru moppuk koṇṭa.
	
	‘cuṭutoḻi larakkarāṟ ṟolinta vāṉuḷōr
 kaṭuvamar kaḷaṉaṭi kalantu kūṟalum
 paṭuporu ḷuṇarntavap paramaṉ yāmiṉi
 yaṭuvatil leṉa maṟuttu.
eṉpuḻi ‘paṭuporu ḷuṇarntavap paramaṉyām’ eṉṟataṉālē avar koḷkiyitu veṉpatiṉitu viḷaṅkum, iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiya cey yuṭkaḷ palavākavum avaṟṟip piṟkālattu viṇavar palar tam mataṉūleṉṟu karuta, māṟṟiyum nīkkiyum varampaḻit - toḻintaṉar. itaṟkuc cāṉṟu acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttiyuḷḷa av virāmayaṇap piratikkum ēṭṭup piratikkum pāṭamovvāmi yiṉṟuṅ kāṇalām. aṉṟiyum, accoṟkaḷum aṭikaḷum ceyyuṭkaḷum ēṟiyuṅ kuṟintum māṟiyum citintum uḷḷavaṟṟik kaṇkūṭākak kāṇalām. ivviṭattataṉi yeṭuttuk kāṭṭappukiṉ viriyumātaliṉ iṭam peṟṟuḻik kāṭṭuvām. iṉic.
	‘cuṭutoḻi larakkarāṟ ṟolinta vāṉuḷōr’
eṉṟa ceyyuḷil.
	‘paṭuporu ḷuṇarntavap paramaṉ yāmiṉi
 aṭuvatil leṉamaṟut tavari yēviṉāṉ’
eṉṟa pāṭatti māṟṟi, ‘maṟuttavarō ṭēkiṉāṉ’ eṉavum, ‘eḻuntaṉar kaṭkaḷiṟṟiṟiyun tāmaric ceḻun tavicakantat tēvum’ eṉṟati māṟṟi ‘eḻuntaṉar kaṟimiṭaṟṟiṟi’ eṉavum citittu vaḻaṅkiṉar. iṅṅaṉam acciṭu muṉṉē āṉavi pala, piṉṉiṭṭā lāṉavaṟṟiṟku varampilli. paṭalap peyarkaḷiyē muṟṟum varampaḻiya māṟṟiṉar. irāmēcurappaṭalatti muṟṟumeṭut teṟintoḻittaṉar. ippaṭalam vāṉmīka virāmāyaṇattilliyē yeṉiṉ, iraṇiyavatip paṭalamum avvāṟē avvāṉmīka virāmāyaṇattilli, yākavē ataṉiyumoḻittal vēṇṭum. iṉṉu miv viraṇiyaṉ vatip paṭalattuḷ.
	‘tāmarik kaṇṇaṉ ṟaḻaṟkaṇṇaṉ pēravi tavira
 nāman taṉṉatē yulakaṅkaḷ yāviyu navila’
eṉṟa ceyyuḷit tammatam paṟṟit ‘tāmarit taṭaṅkaṇṇi ṉāṉ pēravi tavira, nāman taṉṉatē navila’ eṉa māṟṟiṉāru muḷar. iḵtoṉṟō? piramaṉāti yēṉivāṉōr vaṇaṅkiyapaṭi kūṟuvataṉṟic, civaṉ tirumāli vaṇaṅkiyatākak kampar yāṇṭuṅ kūṟiya tilli yeṉpatu naṉkuṇarntum, tammaṉañ cāṉṟu kūṟavum, aṅṅaṉam ammatam paṟṟi yacciṭṭa piratikaḷiliyamuṟṟuc cōtippāṉ pukkavaḻip, paḻiya vēṭṭup piratikaḷōṭu māṟupaṭṭuk kēṭṭup piratiyākavē kiṭantirukkak kaṇṭōm. iccitivu pālakāṇṭam ayōttiyā kāṇṭam āraṇiya kāṇṭattiṉum; kiṭkinti kāṇṭattum cuntarakāṇṭattum mikuti; ivviraṇṭiṉu muyutta kāṇṭam peruṅkēṭuṟṟatu. eṉceyvām; kaṭavuḷ ceyvaki yaṟiyōm. atu niṟka.
“iṉi avvuyutta kāṇṭa morumatat tirutiṟattā racciṭṭa piratikaḷuḷ, oṉṟu parampariyēṭṭup piratikaḷōṭu ciṟitu oṟṟumi yuḷḷatu; maṟṟoṉṟō pala māṟupāṭṭōṭuṅ kūṭi yuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉam tēyam, moḻi (pāṣi), nūl, cāti, camayam, tarumam, mātar, tiraviyam, kuḻavikaḷ mutaliya poruḷkaḷellām, tammatu eṉṟu apimāṉittuk kākkun talivaṉillāvaḻi, iyaṟki tirintu keṭum eṉpatu yāvaru muṇarntapaṭi yaṉṟō?”
(pakkam 22-25. ceṉṉit turittaṉa kalvic cālit tamiḻp pulami, camaraca vēta caṉmārkka caṅkam srī toḻuvūr vēlāyuta mutaliyār eḻutiya mukavuri. ēreḻupatu, tirukki vaḻakkam Memorial Press, Madras. 1886.)











ekara okara eḻuttukkaḷiṉ varivaṭiva varalāṟu
(innūl 194-ām pakkattil kūṟiya ekara okara eḻuttu eṉpatu paṟṟiya toṭarpuri)
eḻuttukkaḷ ellām ekkālattilum orē vitamāka eḻutappaṭavilli. vevvēṟu kālattil vevvēṟu vitamāka eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ivveḻuttukkaḷil ekara okara eḻuttukkaḷiṉ varivaṭivaṅkaḷ evvāṟellām eḻutappaṭṭaṉa eṉpati iṅku ārāyvōm.
inta eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uruva amippip paṟṟi ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷilē cūttiraṅkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mey eḻuttukkaḷum ekara okara uyireḻuttukkaḷum puḷḷipeṟum eṉṟu ac cūttiraṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
	meyyiṉ iyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal
ekara okarat tiyaṟkiyum aṟṟē.
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāram 14, 15-ām cūttiraṅkaḷām.
ki.pi. 11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil puttamittiraṉār iyaṟṟiya vīracōḻiyam eṉṉum ilakkaṇam.
	ekara okara meyyiṟ puḷḷimēvum.
eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. (eḻuttatikāram 3-ām ceyyuḷ.)
ki.pi. 13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil pavaṇanti muṉivar iyaṟṟiya naṉṉūl ilakkaṇam.
tolli vaṭiviṉ ellā eḻuttum āṇṭu eytum ekara okara mey puḷḷi.
eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. (naṉṉūl, eḻuttiyal 43-ām cūttiram.)
ivvilakkaṇac cūttiraṅkaḷiṉpaṭi kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷākiya ekara okaraṅkaḷ, meyyeḻuttukkaḷip pōlavē puḷḷiyiṭ ṭeḻutappaṭa vēṇṭum.
e o ke ko ṅe ṅō ce co ñe ño ṭe
ṭo ṇe ṇo te to mutaliyaṉa. ik kālattu ivveḻuttukkaḷ e o ke ko ṅe ṅō ce co ñe ño ṭe ṭo ṇe ṇo te to eṉṟu eḻutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
paḻiya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ ellām orē puḷḷiyik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyilē iruntavarākiya vīramāmuṉivar eṉṉum ittāli tēcattavar, tām iyaṟṟiya toṉṉūl viḷakkam eṉṉum ilakkaṇa nūlilē.
	“nīṭṭal kaḻittal
 kuṟiṉmeyk kirupuḷḷi”
eṉṟu cūttiram ceytār. nīṇṭa puḷḷi eṉṟum kaḻitta puḷḷi eṉṟum iraṇṭuvita puḷḷiyik kūṟukiṟār. ekara okarak kuṟṟeḻuttukku nīṇṭa puḷḷiyiyum meyyeḻuttukkaḷukkuk cuḻitta puḷḷiyiyum amikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār utāraṇam eri, oti, maṇ kaṇ āṉāl ivar kūṟiya muṟiyi, perumpāṉmiyōr eṟṟukkoṇṭatākat teriyavilli.
1828-ām āṇṭu veḷivanta tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyār  iyaṟṟiya ilakkaṇa viṉāviṭi eṉṉum nūlilē itaṉi naṉku viḷakkukiṟār:
“ivveḻuttukkaḷuḷ vēṟupāṭillāmal camāṉa vaṭivāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa veḻuttukaṭku vēṟupāṭaṟiyu maṭiyāḷametu?
e o eṉṉu muyirk kuṟṟeḻuttum ivviraṇṭu mēṟiṉa ke ko mutaliya uyirmeyk kuṟṟeḻuttum k ṅ mutaliya meyyeḻuttum mēlē. ippaṭiyoru puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutappaṭum. varalāṟu.
evaṉ. oppu, keṇṭi, koṇṭi-ivikaḷukkuḷḷē meyyeḻuttukkum puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutiṉami kāṇka.”
meyyeḻuttukkaḷip pōlavē ekara okara eḻuttukkaḷ paṇṭik kālattil puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutappaṭṭaṉa eṉpataṟkuk kīḻkkaṇṭa paḻiya ceyyuṭkaḷum cāṉṟākum.
	“nēriḻiyār kūntaliṉ or puḷḷipeṟa nīḷmaramām
 nīrnili or puḷḷipeṟa neruppām”
nēriḻiyār (peṇkaḷiṉ) kūntalukku oti eṉpatu peyar. ōti eṉpatu paṇṭik kālattil, ilakkaṇa muṟippaṭiyum vaḻakka muṟippaṭiyum oti eṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭatu. itil uḷḷa okaram okāramākum. itaṉ mēl oru puḷḷi iṭṭu oti eṉṟu ippaṭi eḻutiṉāl, ōkārattiṉ oru māttiri kuṟintu okaramāki oti eṉṟu paṭikkappaṭum. oti eṉpatu oru marattiṉ peyar (oti maram). nīr nilikkup peyar ēri eṉpatu. itu paṇṭik kālattil, ilakkaṇa muṟippaṭiyum vaḻakka muṟippaṭiyum eri eṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭatu. inta ekarattiṉmēl oru puḷḷiyiṭṭu eri eṉṟu ippaṭi eḻutiṉāl, ēkāramākiya neṭṭeḻuttu oru māttiri kuṟintu eri eṉṟu paṭikkappaṭum. eri eṉṟāl neruppu. itittāṉ ic ceyyuḷ.
	‘nēriḻiyār kūntaliṉ or puḷḷipeṟa nīḷmaramām,
 nīrnili or puḷḷipeṟa neruppām.”
eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
ekara okara uyirmey yeḻuttukkaḷukku vīramāmuṉivar putiya vaṭivaṅkaḷi amittār. paṇṭik kālattil ke, ke; ṅe, ṅe; ce ce; ñe, ñe; eṉṟu ivvāṟu eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ivi muṟiyē ke, kē; ṅe, ṅē;ce, cē; ñe, ñē eṉṟu paṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟē ko, ko; ṅō, ṅō; co, co; ño, ño; eṉṟu mivvāṟu eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ivi muṟiyē ko, kō; no, ṅo; co, cō; ño ñō eṉṟu paṭikkap paṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu kuṟṟeḻuttukkuk kompiṉmēl puḷḷi vittum neṭṭeḻuttukkuk kompiṉmēl puḷḷi vikkāmalum eḻutappaṭṭati māṟṟi, vīramā muṉivar kompiṉmēl puḷḷi vikkāmalviṭṭāl kuṟṟeḻuttākavum kompi mēlē cuḻittāl neṭṭeḻuttākavum paṭikkavēṇṭu meṉṟu putitāka oru muṟiyamittār.
itippaṟṟi vīramā muṉivar tām lattiṉ moḻiyil eḻutiya ‘koṭuntamiḻ’ ilakkaṇattiṉ mutal atikārattil ivvāṟu eḻutukiṟār. ataṉ tamiḻ ākkam varumāṟu:
“ekarak kuṟṟeḻuttum ēkāra neṭṭeḻuttum, okarak kuṟṟeḻuttum okāra neṭṭeḻuttum, kuṟil neṭil vēṟupāṭukaḷik kāṭṭum aṭiyāḷam illāmal, orē vitamāka eḻutap paṭuvatāl, tamiḻar kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṉum vēṟupāṭṭiṉip pirittaṟiyum poruṭṭu, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷiṉmēl puḷḷi vittum neṭṭeḻuttiṉmēl puḷḷi vikkāmalum eḻutumpaṭi kaṟpik kiṟārkaḷ. ākavē, mey eṉṉum collil uḷḷa kompiṉmēl puḷḷi vikkātapaṭiyāl mēy eṉṟu paṭikkappaṭukiṟatu. mey eṉṉum collil kompiṉmēl puḷḷi vittirukkiṟapaṭiyāl atu mey eṉṟu paṭikkappaṭukiṟatu. appaṭiyē poy eṉṉum collil kompu puḷḷi peṟātapaṭiyāl pōy eṉṟu paṭikkappaṭukiṟatu. poy eṉpatil kompu puḷḷi peṟṟiruppataṉāl poy eṉṟu paṭikkappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl, intap puḷḷikaḷ vittu eḻutuvati nāṉ perumpālum pārttatilli. iḵtu  ēṭeḻutu vōriṉ acaṭṭittaṉamāka irukkalām. inta ekara okarak kuṟṟe ḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭṭi eḷitākat terintu koḷḷutaṟku vēṟoru vakiyi nāṉ kaṇṭaṟintēṉ eṉpatit terivittukkoḷkiṟēṉ. itu piṉvarumāṟu.
kuṟilukkum neṭilukkum orē mātiriyāka eḻutappaṭukiṟa kompu eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟa b inta eḻuttik kuṟṟeḻuttuk kākavum, intak kompiyē mēlē cuḻittu n ivvitamāka eḻutuvati neṭṭeḻut tukkākavum amittēṉ. utāraṇamāka mey, mēy poy, pōy. inta muṟi oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭu anēkarāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.”
ivvāṟu ekara okara uyirmey yeḻuttukkaḷukkup putiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷi vīramāmuṉivar amittapaṭiyē ippōtu nām eḻutivarukiṟōm. 1828-ām āṇṭil ilakkaṇa viṉāviṭi eḻutiya tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyār.

	“vēṟu palar kōṭu (kompu) peṟṟa neṭi
luyirmeykaḷik kōṭṭuṭcuḻittu meḻutuvar. 
varalāṟu: kol-kōl eṉa vaḻaṅkum.”
eṉṟu vīramā muṉivar amitta kuṟiyīṭṭik kāṭṭiṉār.
vīramā muṉivar amitta ikkuṟiyīṭukaḷ, ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē ellōrālum payilappaṭṭapaṭiyiṉālē
	“ekara okara meypuḷḷi peṟum”
eṉṉum paḻiya ilakkaṇac cūttiram māṟṟiyamikkap paṭavēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. ākavē. iyaṟṟamiḻāciriyar irāmānuca kavirāyar tāmiyaṟṟiya naṉṉūṟ kāṇṭikiyi 1847-ām āṇṭu acciyaṟṟiyapōtu iccūttirattit talikīḻāka māṟṟiyamittār.
	“tollivaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum āṇṭu
 eytum ēkāram okāram mey puḷḷi”
eṉṟu cūttiratti māṟṟiṉār. māṟṟiyataṟku ivvāṟu viḷakkaṅ kūṟiṉār:
tollāciriyar mutalāyiṉōr,
	“paḻiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṇa pukutalum
 vaḻuvala kāla vakiyiṉāṉē”
eṉavum.
	“iṟantatu vilakkal etiratu pōṟṟal”
eṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamiyāl, ekara mokara mey puḷḷipeṟum eṉṟa iccūttiratti ēkāra mōkāra mey puḷḷi peṟum eṉat tiruppa vēṇṭiṟṟu eṉṉeṉiṉ, ikkālattār ēkāra okāraṅkaḷukkē puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutuvatu peruvaḻakkāyiṉamiyā leṉka.”
irāmānuca kavirāyar, ēkāram okāram puḷḷi peṟum eṉṟu kūṟuvatu, ēkāra okāra uyirmey eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kompukaḷ mēlē cuḻikkappaṭuvatiyē.
āṉāl, paḻiya ilakkaṇa muṟippaṭi ekara ēkāra okara ōkāraṅkaḷ e e o ō eṉṟu eḻutappaṭa vēṇṭiyavi. ik kālattu e ē o ō eṉṟu ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉ puḷḷi eṭupaṭṭu ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ kīḻē nīṇṭa puḷḷiyiyum vaṭṭa puḷḷiyiyum peṟṟatu eppōtu yārāl eṉpatu teriyavilli.
	“nīṭṭal cuḻittal
 kuṟiṉ meyk kirupuḷḷi”
eṉṟu vīramā muṉivar tamatu “toṉṉūl viḷakkam” eṉṉum nūlil cūttiram iyaṟṟiṉār. itaṟku utāraṇam eri, oti, maṇa, kaṇa eṉpaṉa. nīṇṭa puḷḷiyi ekara okarak kuṟṟeḻuttukku iṭavēṇṭum eṉṟum cuḻitta puḷḷiyi meyyeḻuttukkiṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟum amittār. itil nīṇṭa puḷḷi, cuḻitta puḷḷi eṉpatu tavira maṟṟappaṭi paḻiya ilakkaṇattukku māṟupaṭṭataṉṟu: avaṟṟi yoṭṭiyatē. āṉāl ekara okarattiṟku mēṟ puḷḷiyi māṟṟi ēkāra okārattukkuk kīḻē nīṇṭa kōṭṭiyum cuḻitta puḷḷiyiyum amittatu yār? eppoḻutu? eṉpatu ārāyaṟpālatu.
ēkāraneṭ ṭeḻuttukkuk kīḻkkōṭu amittavar vīramā muṉivar eṉṟu kūṟuvar. (Burnell’s South Indian Paleography P. 5.)







cāṟṟuk kavikaḷ
ōr āciriyar putitāka nūl iyaṟṟiṉāl annūlukkup pāyiram amippatu paṇṭik kālattu vaḻakkam. ippōtum avvaḻakkam uṇṭu. pāyiram illāta nūl nūlaṉṟu. nūlukkup pāyiram iṉṟi yamiyātatu.
	“āyiram mukattāṉ akaṉṟa tāyiṉum
 pāyiram illatu paṉuval aṉṟē”
eṉpatu cūttiram. pāyiram vaḻaṅkuvōr nūlāciriyariṉ āciriyarum, avaruṭaṉ payiṉṟavarum, avar māṇākkarum ākiya mūvaril oruvarāka iruppar. nūlukku uri eḻutum uriyāciriyarum pāyiram kūṟuvar.
ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē, ēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyāka irunta paḻaya nūlkaḷi accup puttakamāka acciṭṭavarum, putitāka nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟi acciṟ patippittōrum, tammuṭaṉ payiṉṟavar, tam naṇpar, tam āciriyar, tamatu māṇavar mutaliyavarkaḷiṭamiruntu cāṟṟuk kavikaḷip peṟṟut tamatu nūlil acciṭṭaṉar. 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil nūlkaḷi acciṭṭa pulavarkaḷ perumpālōrum tam nūlkaḷil cāṟṟuk kavikaḷi acciṭṭaṉar. cāṟṟuk kavikaḷ illāta nūlkaḷ ak kālattil mikaccila. cāṟṟukkavi acciṭum vaḻakkam inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkam variyil iruntuvantatu. piṟaku aṭiyōṭu maṟintu pōyiṟṟu. cāṟṟuk kaviyic ciṟappup pāyiram eṉṟum kūṟuvar.
cāṟṟuk kavi eṉpatu cāttuk kavi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. cāṟṟuk kavi cāttuk kavi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ cārttukkavi eṉpataṉ marūupōlum. cāṟṟuk kavikaḷ ellām ceyyuḷākavē iruntaṉa. akkālattil pulavarkaḷ ellōrum ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟa aṟinti runtaṉar. ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟat teriyātavar akkālattil pulavarākap, paṇṭitarākak karutappaṭṭilar.  niṉittavuṭaṉ eḷitākac ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟun tiṟaṉ vāyntirunta avarkaḷ naṉṟāka vacaṉam eḻuta aṟiyātavarkaḷ. ikkālattil, ataṟku nērmāṟāka, vacaṉam eḻutat terintavarukkuc ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟa muṭikiṟatilli.
cāṟṟuk kavikaḷip paṭippataṉ mūlamāka nūlippaṟṟiyum āciriyarip paṟṟiyum uriyāciriyarip paṟṟiyum patippāciriyarip paṟṟiyum pala ceytikaḷ aṟintukoḷḷa muṭikiṟatu. aṉṟiyum cāṟṟuk kavi vaḻaṅkiyavarkaḷip paṟṟiyum terintukoḷḷa vāyppuk kiṭikkiṟatu. itaṟku mēlāka, orē kālattil irunta pulavarkaḷit terintukoḷḷa muṭikiṟatu. inta nūli eḻutuvataṟku ak kālattuc cāṟṟuk kavikaḷ eṉakkup peritum utaviyāka iruntaṉa.
ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷivanta nūlkaḷilē uḷḷa cāṟṟuk kavikaḷi yellām tokuttu  acciṭṭāl atu perunūlāka amiyum. ataṉāl pulavarkaḷ varalāṟum ilakkiyaṅ kaḷiṉ varalāṟum ēṉiya ceytikaḷum aṟivataṟku muṭiyum utāraṇa mākac cila kāṭṭuvōm.
tirukkōvalūr āṟumuka cuvāmikaḷ “tiṭṭānupūti’ eṉṉum nūli iyaṟṟiṉār. annūlukkuc ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta muttukkiruṣṇa piramam eṉpavar uri eḻuti avvuriyuṭaṉ annūli 1875-ām āṇṭil acciṟ patippittār. antap patippukku ak kālattil avaruṭaṉ paḻakiya pala pulavarkaḷ cāṟṟuk kavi vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷpeyar varumāṟu:
puraci aṣṭāvatāṉam capāpati mutaliyār, citamparam irāmaliṅkam piḷḷi, (aruṭpā irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ), cēmaṅkalam vittuvāṉ nārāyaṇa mutaliyār, tirunīrmali kōpālakiruṣṇa kavirāja cēkarar, tilliyampūrc cantiracēkara kavirāja paṇṭitar, naraciṅkapuram tamiḻp pulami vīrācāmi mutali yār, tāyumāṉa cuvāmikaḷ marapil eḻuntaruḷiya eṭuttukkūṭṭi aruṇācala cuvāmikaḷ, kuḷattūrk karatala mūrttiyākiya caṭṭanāta cuvāmikaḷ, cēṅkālipuram kōyiṟpattu kuḻantivēlu otuvār, puraci aruṇācala cuvāmikaḷ māṇākkarākiya kā. āṟumuka nāyakar, ceṉṉi iṅkilīṣ upāttiyāyar mayili kurucāmi mutaliyār, tañci āṟumukap piramam, tañci i. kuppucāmi nāyakar, koṅkuṇappaṭṭi tamiḻp paḷḷik kūṭattu upāttiyāyar a. cēṣap piḷḷi, tamiḻp paḷḷikkūṭam kōvinta upāttiyāyar, tāṉappa kavuṇṭar, ciṉṉappāvu mūrttiyār, nārāyaṇap piramam ākiyōr, ivvuri nūlukkuc ciṟappup pāyiram vaḻaṅkiyavar, vittuvacikāmaṇiyākiya aṣṭāvatāṉam vīrācāmi ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷ. itaṉāl ivarkaḷ orē kālattil iruntavar eṉpatu terikiṉṟatu.
vallūr tēvarācap piḷḷiyavarkaḷ cūtacaṅkiti eṉṉum nūli moḻipeyarttu 1856-ām āṇṭu acciṟ patippittār. inta nūlukkup palar ciṟappup pāyiram vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ peyar varumāṟu:
tiricirapuram vittuvāṉ mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷi, koṭṭiyūr civakkoḻuntu tēcikar kumārar cāmināta tēcikar, nākaveḷip pāḷiyam vaḷḷal cantāṉam caṇmuka tēcikar, tañci aṇṇāmalip piḷḷi, tiricirapuram vīrarākava ceṭṭiyār, makāvittuvāṉ tiricirapuram mīṉāṭci cuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ māṇākkaruḷoruvarum upāttiyāyar tiricirapuram citamparam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ kumārarumākiya cuntarampiḷḷi, cōṭacāvatāṉam cupparāya ceṭṭiyār, tiyākarāja ceṭṭiyār, tiricirapuram kuppamuttāppiḷḷi, tiricirapuram vittuva cirōmaṇi mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ māṇākkariloruvarākiya tiruvīḻimiḻali irāmāyaṇap piracaṅkam cāminātak kavirāyar, tārā nallūr vīrāccāmi nāyakar, tiricirapuram appāvup piḷḷi eṉkiṟa muttucāmip piḷḷi, kempuvēli aḻakiricāmi nāyakar, kōvintacāmip piḷḷi, tiricirapuram murukap piḷḷi, tiricirapuram nīlakaṇṭappiḷḷi, tiricirapuram caravaṇa upāttiyāyar, uṟiyūr ciṉṉiyap pulavar, maturi vīrapattirap piḷḷi, vitvacirōmaṇi kāñcīpuram kaccapāliya aiyar, āṟimānakar muttamiḻk kavi tiruvēṅkaṭap piḷḷi, āṟimānakar aiyācāmi mutaliyār, kavitva paricīlaka kajakaṇṭīravam ā. namaccivāya mutaliyār, kaliyāṇapura meṉṉum peṅkaḷūr caturvēta cintānta capiyic cērnta tamiḻ vittiyācāli mutalupāttiyāyar, kāñcīpuram irāmānuja piḷḷi, meyyūr poṉṉampala nāyakar, kāñcīpuram pāratap piracaṅkam cāmiyareṉkiṟa irakunātayyar, puṉṉippākkam cupparāya mutaliyār, taṇṭalam citampara mutaliyār, kāñcīpuram ciṅkāravēlu piḷḷi. itaṉāl ivar ellōrum camakālattil iruntavar eṉpatu terikiṉṟatu.
tāṇṭavarāya cuvāmikaḷ iyaṟṟiya kivalliya navanītam eṉṉum nūlukkut tirutturutti intirapīṭam karapāttira cuvāmikaḷ ātīṉattu īcūr caccitāṉanta cuvāmikaḷ uri eḻuti 1889-ām āṇṭu acciṟ patippittār. inta uri nūlukkuc cāṟṟukkavi pāṭiṉōr peyar varumāṟu:
ceṉṉic carvakalācālit tamiḻt talimip paṇṭitarākiya iyaṟṟamiḻāciriyar kō. irājakōpāla piḷḷi, tirumayili vitvāṉ caṇmukam piḷḷi, tiricirapuram kālēj tamiḻp paṇṭitar a. ka. amirtaliṅkam piḷḷi, tiruvallikkēṇi vēṅkaṭācalap pulavar, cēlañ cillā vikīl attuvitāṉupavi appācuvāmi piḷḷi, tirumūlar marapu catācivap piramayōkiyiṉ māṇākkar 
tē. cupparāya mutaliyār, cēlam kōmaḷēcuvara mutaliyār, srīmat cuvakīyāṉantap piramayōkīntirar māṇākkarum atvita vivēka camāja stāpakarumākiya maṇimaṅkalam tiruvēṅkaṭa mutaliyār, mēṟpaṭiyār māṇākkarākiya vāyalūr capāpati mutaliyār. ivarkaḷ ellōrum oru kālattavar.
ivvāṟu pala nūlkaḷiliruntu cāṟṟuk kavi vaḻaṅkiyavar kaḷik kāṭṭalām. virivañci niṟuttukiṟōm. intac cāṟṟuk kavikaḷiliruntu antanta nūl veḷiyiṭṭa āciriyarkaḷiṉ kālattilirunta pulavarkaḷ peyariyum avarkaḷiṉ toḻil ūr mutaliyavaṟṟiyum terintu koḷkiṟōm.
nūlkaḷukkuc ciṟappup pāyiram allatu cāṟṟuk kavikaḷi acciṭum vaḻakkam ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iruntatu. piṉṉar avvaḻakkam 20-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilum iruntatu. piṟaku niṉṟuviṭṭatu.
cāṟṟuk kavikaḷiṟ civaṟṟi iṅkut tarukiṉṟēṉ. 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷivanta nūlkaḷiṉ cāṟṟuk kavikaḷi yellām kāṭṭiṉāl atuvē periya nūlākiviṭum. ākiyāl mātirikkāka oru cilavaṟṟi maṭṭum tarukiṟēṉ.


nītinūl
māyūram vētanāyakam piḷḷi avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya nītinūlukkut tiruttaṇiki vicākap perumāḷiyaravarkaḷiyaṟṟiya cāṟṟuk kavi. iccāṟṟuk kavi 1892-ām āṇṭu ca. ñāṉappirakācam piḷḷiyavarkaḷāl acciṭappaṭṭa nītinūl mūlamum uriyum eṉṉum puttakattil uḷḷatu.
nilamaṇṭila vāciriyappā
	mutaṉaṭu vīṟilā muṉivaṉ paṭitta
natipati yuṭutta ñālat tuṟivōr
peṟutaṟ kuriya perumpaya ṉivi yeṉ
ṟaṟitaru curuti miruti yātiya
veṇmi yaṉṟuṇarva teṉamuṉi vararu		5
muṇmivaḷ ḷuvaru muṟṟiyuḷ ḷiraṅku
paṟamuṉ muppā lākavap poruḷit
tiṟaṉuṟat tamiḻiṟ ṟeḷḷitiṉ vakutta
māṇpami nūloṭu caṅka marīiya
vēṇko ṇāvalarka ḷiyātta paṉṉūlu		10
naṉkiṉi tāyntu nayanterin tevarkku
maṅkiyiṟ kaṉipō liyantiri porīip
pulappaṭac coṟcuvi poruṭcuvi potuḷa
vilakkaṇak kaviñari yāvarum viyantu
muṭituḷak kuṟuvāṉ kaṭikamaḻ tēṟaluṅ		15
kaṉṉalu mamutuṅ kalantu kuḻittāṅ
kiṉṉici tamīiya paṉṉaruñ ciṟappi
ṉaṇikeḻu ceyyuḷāṉ maṇivaṭa mēyppac
cālpuṭi nīti nūloṉ ṟiyaṟṟiṉa
ṉaḷantaṟi voṇṇā valaṅkuḻūu vuṟṟa		20
tāṭaka vulakeṉu matupaṭ ṭāṅkuko
ṉāṭutu meṉṉā vōṭi nimirnteṉa
vuyarvaṟa vuyarnta puyaṟavaḻ kuṭumip
poṉmā ṇeyilum paṉmā ḷikiyum
viḷaṅkic ceṟinta kuḷantatik kiṟivaṉ		25
pavanteṟu kaṅkā kulapari pālaṉ
ṟavantaru poṉṉaṇi yeṉuntaṇ ṇatiyōṉ
centēṉ pilku pintārk kuvaḷiyaṉ
ṟiciyiru nāṉkiṉuñ ceṉṟu kavintu
vaciyaṟa vōṅki maliperum pukaḻōṉ		30
ṟaṉṉuyi rāmeṉat taṇṇaḷi purintē
maṉṉuyirt tokuti malkap purappō
ṉāḻi peyarvuṟ ṟakilañ citippa
vūḻi peyariṉu muripiṟa ḻātō
ṉakavi mūṉṟuḷa makavoṉ ṟiṉukkup		35
perumalip pāvi tirumalip pāṟaruñ
cēṇuyar cōlit tōṇi puratti
loṭṭār naṭṭā rulaki layaleṉap
paṭṭā reṉuman tarampā rāmē
nāṭun tulipō ṉaṭuniṉ ṟuykkum		40
pīṭu peṟunaṟ pirāṭṭu vivāka
ṉāti nāyaka ṉaṭipaṇin tēttum
vēta nāyakaṉā mikaval lōṉē.
āciriyac canta viruttam
	tāru lāliya moympaṉāṭaka tārnērtaru ceṅkiyāṉ
ṟāḻvi lāvurumāraṉ viñcu tayāḷa ṉaṇputaḻintuḷāṉ
nēru lāvu patākiyā ṉulakēḻu nīṭici taṅkuvāṉ
yāvu māyiṇi yoṉṟilā toḷirēka tēvi
                                                 yiṟiñcuvāṉ.
nīru lāvu taṭāka meṅku nilāvu kiṉṟa kuḷantivāḻ
nirupaṉu yirnati marapu taṉilvaru nipuṇaṉ varakuṇa
                                                      ciṅkamāñ
cīru lāviya vēta nāyaka cīlaṉ nīti ciṟanta tōr
teḷiyu nūliṉi yutaviṉāṉmati teḷiñar yārum
                                                     viyakkavē.

ilakkaṇac curukka viṉāviṭi
ilakkaṇac curukka viṉā viṭiyit tiruttaṇiki vicākapperumāḷiyar 1828-il eḻutiṉār. innūliṉ ciṟappup pāyiram, innūlāciriyark kiḷavalākiya tiruttaṇiki caravaṇap perumā ḷiyarāṟ collappaṭṭatu.
	“nilampeṟu nararkkaru ṇilimutaṟ kaṭavuḷ
 palampeṟa varuḷiya paṟpala moḻikaḷuḷ
 vaṭakkiṉmā vēṅkaṭa variyun teṟkoṭu
 kuṭakkiṟ kuṇakki ṉīḷkutiri nāliyu
 melli yeṉattika ḻiṭattu moḻiyāki		5
 yilli yitaṟkiṇi yeṉakkeḻu takimiyiṉ
 vaḻuttaṟ ciṟappoṭu vaḷartami ḻilakkaṇa
 meḻuttuc coṟporuḷ yāppaṇi yintu
 nūṟpā vilumavi naṉuku miṉattilu
 nūṟpā vilumpeṟa nuvaṉṟuḷa valakila		10
 voṉṟā yiṉumami vuṟavuṇar taṟkeḷi
 taṉṟē kūriya rallārk kātaliṉ
 yāvaru muṇaravav vintiyuñ curukki
 mēvarum viṉāvum viṭiyu mākat
 takavi ṉalluri vācakatti ṉavilkeṉāc		15
 cukavaḷa niṟitaru toṇṭanā ṭataṉiṟ
 kaccik kīriṅ kaṭikiyiṉ vaṭakīḻp
 pacciḷañ cāli parūukkaḻi yāmeṉak
 kaḻikamu kāmeṉak kalimaṭaṟ pāḷit
 taḻitaru kamuku tāḻiyā meṉamē		20
 leḻuntuyarn taṇmiṉōrk kiṟumpūtu viḷikkuṅ
 koḻumpuṉaṉ marutak kūvamā nakarō
 ṉōmak kaṉalika lukapporu ciṉakkaṉa
 lēmak kaṉalceyu mivarkkumaṟ revarkku
 mutarak kaṉalavit tōmpuṟu vaḷaṅkūr		25
 maturat tilakuṇā vakiceya lāṉā
 vaṟaṉkaṭik kaḻivukā ṇarumpeṟa luḻavut
 tiṟaṉkaṭip piṭittiṭu tiruttaku kulattiṉaṉ
 kaḻimoḻi maṅkiyar kaṇṇeṉum pakiyiṉam
 viḻiyara ṇāmivaṇ mēvip porutumeṉ	30
 ṟaṇiniri yuṟṟirun tāṅkupiṅ kuvaḷi
 maṇimala roliyalār vaṇpuya variyiṉa
 ṉuruvoṭu miruntikaḻ vuṟāti vaṉmuṉa
 maruva meytiyatu naṉṟāyateṉ ṟanaṅkaṉ
 karutiyuḷat taḻukkaṟuppa nōkki ṉarkkelā		35
 miruviḻik kūṇeṉu meḻiṉiṟi yuruviṉaṉ
 ṟakkavar nāppaṇōr tāṉciṟan tuṟumavi
 yokkumeṉa viliyuyar palamaṇikaḷi
 ṉaṭuva ṇoruperu nāyaka maṇiyuṭic
 cuṭarviri matāṇi tuyalvaru mārpiṉaṉ		40
 muttamiḻp pulamiyiṉ muntiya nāvalar
 puttamiḻ teṉaccuvi poḻitara vakamalarn
 tukantutaṟ pukaḻnta vopparum pāṭṭoṭu
 tikantamō reṭṭiluñ ceṉṟoḷir cīrttiya
 ṉiruvicum piṟivaṉuk kiṟivaṉā talumoru	45
 poruḷeṉā teṇkuṇap puṉitaṉem pacupati
 yaṭiyavark kaṭiyava ṉātalē poruḷeṉa
 voṭivaṟa vuḷatteḻun tōṅkuṟu maṉpiṉaṉ
 tirumēṉi vaḷḷal ceyperun tavattā
 lorumēṉi koṇṭiva ṇutittacev vēḷeṉap 50
 poruntōki mēluṟīip pulavarip pōṟṟit
 taruntiṟaṉ ñāṉa caktiyit tāṅki
 yaṅkamala vētaṉic ceruk kaṭakki
 maṅkalil periya variyaṭut tuṟivō
 ṉāviṉ kiḻatti nayantuṟi pavarvayiṟ		55
 pūviṉ kiḻatti pukāḷeṉa lēṉi
 yarukkā mivaṉṟaṉak kaṉṟeṉa viḷaṅkun
 tirukkāmi māluri ceytaṉa ṉāka
 nayattaku tolkāp piyanaṉ ṉūṉmuta
 liyaṟṟamiḻ nūlkaḷi liṉiyaṉa palataḻīi		60
 tiṭamuḷa vaḻakkoṭu ceyyuḷiṟ cilataḻīi
 vaṭanūl viticila varattaku vaṉakoḷīi
 ilakkaṇac curukka viṉāviṭi yeṉumpeyar
 nalattiyi taraniṟīi naṭiyuṟa vakuttaṉaṉ
 cīrpeṟu teṉṟirut taṇikivāḻ celvaṉ	65
 cārpuṟu vīra civa cikāmaṇi
 malakaruṭ kuṟiyuḷ varakuṇa mēru
 kalvip peruṅkaṭal kantappa tēcikaṉ
 aruntavap perumpē ṟāmeṉa vutitta
 peruntaki vicākap perumā ḷiyaṉeṉ		70
 ṟulaku pukaḻtara vōṅki
 ilaku miyaṟpeya reytu nāvalaṉē.”
naṉṉūṟ kāṇṭiki yuri
tiruttaṇiki vicākapperumāḷiyar avarkaḷ naṉṉūluk kuk kāṇṭiki uri eḻuti acciṭṭapōtu, ataṟkuc cēyūr muttiya mutaliyār aḷitta urippāyiram:
	pūmali tuṇarcciṉi potuḷiya cantaṉak
kāmali potiyaliṟ kāṭcikūr nalattiṟ
paṟpala muṉivarar paṇiceyū varukuṟac
ciṟpara ṉaṭiyuḷañ cērttirun taṟaneṟi
muyalici yakattiya muṉivaṉiṟ ṟōṉṟiya		5
viyalici nāṭaka meṉumivi pulappaṭa
nutaliya varumpeṟa ṉōkkami yakattiya
mutaliya vāyuḷa muttamiḻ nūlkaḷu
mavaṉmā ṇākkarī raṟuvari ṉōṅkiya
tavaṉmā ṇākkaril cāyttatol kāppiyaṉ	10
vaḻuvā yaṟatterīi vakuttatol kāppiya
meḻuvā yākiya viyaṟṟamiḻ nūlkaḷun
tiṅkaṭ kavikicēr teṉṉava ṉālvaḷar
caṅkap palakivāḻ cāṉṟōrk kaḷaviyāyk
kucinuṉi pōṉmatik kūrmiyōr tamakkē	15
yicitali ṉivipō lātiyā varukkun
teḷiyiya ṉūlkaḷiṟ ciṟantatōr nūluk
keḷituṇa ruriyiṉi tiyaṟṟit tarukeṉāp
pukaḻtaru pāṟkaṭaṟ poṅkavaṅ kataṉiṭit
tikaḻtaru kalivaḷar tiṅkaḷvan tutitteṉa		20
nilamuḻu tōṅkunaṉ ṉilikeḻu viciya
kulamatu makiḻuḷaṅ kūravan tutittōṉ
coṟpala malarkoṭu toṭuttapā māliyu
muṟpala māliyu moḷirmaṇi moympiṉaṉ
paruvamoṉ ṟiṭiyoru payaṉuta veḻilināṇ	25
maruvavep poḻutiṉu maruppala koṭiyiṉa
ṉepporu ḷumpakut tiḵtiṟ ṟāmeṉa
vapporu ḷuṇmitērn taṟiyanuṇ ṇuṇarviṉa
ṉaṭicaci vāṇmuka malaravāy nalattoṭu
puṭivaḷar viputarip pōṟṟivāḻ puṉitaṉ	30
cētu patiyeṉuñ ceṅkōṉ maṉṉavaṟ
kōtunaṟ ṟuṇiyeṉa vuṟṟapūtam pāvi
yaṇṇā cāmivē ḷaruntava meṉkoleṉ
ṟeṇṇāṟ ṟiciyuḷō riyampiṭat tōṉṟiyōṉ
civaṉaṭi yavariyac civaṉeṉap paravun		35
tavaṉaṭi muṭivilāc civa cikāmaṇi
ciṅkāra mutalitaṉ cintikūr makiḻcciyi
ṉiṅkā ramutaṉa viṉcolāl vēṇṭaliṉ
muṉṉū loḻiyap piṉṉūl palaviṉu
ḷennū lāru naṉṉū lāruk			40
kiṇiyō veṉattami ḻiṉituṇar pavarkkelān
tuṇiyī tāmeṉat tōṉṟunaṉ ṉūṟku
nayaṉmiku caṅkara namacci vāyaṉāṟ
payaṉmikac ceytiṭap paṭṭataṉ piṉṉar
tavañā ṉantaṉiṟ cālpukūr tuṟicic		45
civañāṉa muṉivaṉāṟ ṟiruttiṭap paṭṭa
virutti yuritaṉi veḷippaṭac curukkik
karuttup patapporuḷ kāṭṭumaṟ ṟuñcila
vuṟumuṟi kāṇṭiki yuriyuḷaṅ koṇṭu
ciṟuvaru muṇartaruñ cevviyiṟ ceytaṉaṉ	50
ṟattuva muṇartirut taṇiki maṭātipaṉ
catteṉum vīra civamā kēcaṉ
kaṟṟuṇarn tōṅkiya kantappa tēcikaṉ
peṟṟaruḷ vicākap perumā ḷiyaṉeṉ
ṟulaku pukaḻtara vōṅki				55
yilaku miyaṟpeya reytu nāvalaṉē.
tolkāppiya naṉṉūl
1858-ām āṇṭil cāmuvēl eṉpavar tolkāppiya naṉṉūli iyaṟṟi acciṭṭār. ataṟku avaratu iṣṭarākiya puraci aṣṭāvatāṉam capāpati mutaliyāravarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya ciṟappup pāyiram:
	pūmici yakalā nāṉmukat toruvaṉ
paṭippuḻi yiṉcoli ṉiyakkan terippāṉ
irukkiyāy vāḻiya reṉāmē ṉiṟuviya
vēṅkaṭaṅ kumari yōṅkiya  mēlkīḻp
puṇaricūḻ varippi laṇaviya muttamiḻ		5
āṉṟatol kaṭaluṭ ṭōṉṟuṟu mimporuḷ 
īriñ ñūñāṟṟuc cīrami paṇamaṇic
cūṭṭaru ṇeṭṭuṭaṟ kōṭṭamil pāppara
cēntupu kiṭanta māṇpami nīṇilat
tēvaru muṇarāt tāvarum payaṉkoḷa		10
uyirtoṟuñ civaṇiya ceyiraṟu potunaṭa
navilun taṉimutal tavalaruṅ kivalan
tamiḻici katuviya tamarukat teḻumēḻ
iraṭṭiya veḻuttoli teruṭṭiya vāriya
neṭumoḻip pāṇini maṭaṉiku mutiyōṉ		15
pōṉmeṉa viruntamiḻp pāṉmi muḻutuṇar
mukaṭṭuyar potiyap pokuṭṭuḻi yiruntaruḷ
paramā ciriyaṟ paṟpakal vaḻipaṭ
ṭarumpeṟa liyalpeṟīip poruntiya paṉṉiru
māṇavak kuḻāttu ṇīṇili koḷuviya		20
olkāp peruñcīrt tolkāppiya muṉi
taṟpeyar tōṟṟi yaṟpuṟat tanta
aintira niṟinta viyantaku nuṇporuṭ
ṭoṉmicāl kāppiyat tuṇmarīip potivaṉa
pāṭṭuvari peṟumēm pāṭṭuṭi miyiṉil	25
āṉāk kaṅkaṉ mēṉāḷ vēṇṭaliṉ
caṉakā purattu ḷiṉiyāc cālpami
caṉmati tanta vaṇmikūr ciṟappiṟ
pavaṇanti yuravōṉ ṟuvaḷaṟa vakutta
naṉṉū leṉṉu mannūṟ kiṭantaṉa			30
poruḷoru cērat teruḷuṟac cūḻntu
centamiḻ nāṭē yaṉṟiyu māntira
malāṭu karunaṭaṅ kulāvumiv vātit
tēttum vaḻaṅkun tiṟaṉ ṟiṟampāmi
yorumoḻik kuriyava raṉṟiyum viravum		35
aiṅkili yammuta laṟiñaru mēṟka
āriyan tiraviṭa māntiraṅ karunaṭam
oru miṅkiliyat turipotuc ciṟappu
naṭinu taliyaṉa vaṭiviṟ kaṟpavar
palvaki nalaṉaṟi malku muṇarcciyiṉ		40
uḷḷak kiḷarcciyuṅ koḷḷap peṟuvaṉa
nūṉmoḻi peyarkkum vāymik karuviyun
teḷḷiyōr payaṉaṭi cevviyuṭ koḷḷupu
nantiyam poruppi ṉantiyum pakalum
arukā vaḷaṅkeḻūum varuvāyt tiripilāp	45
pārva yiṉiṉpu nīḷvaḻi yutavuṟūum
pāṟpeya rāṟṟoḻuk kēṟpateñ ñāṉṟum
ēṉinā ṭavipō lātiru mikkum
piṟavik kelli kāṇuu niṟivuṟak
kallāp puṇaru nayaṉuṭi nallōr		50
peṭpuṟun tiruviṉa toṭpamār toṇṭi
naṭṭāṭ ṭiṭinīḷ paṉmāṇ pukaḻmi
maṉṉuteṇ ṭiricūḻ ceṉṉiyam patikka
ṇiṟikoḷum pulamit tuṟivantavartaṅ
kuḻuvakaṅ kavarā makiḻtali ciṟappa			55
orutāṉ kaṇṭavi piṟarun terikeṉu
nīkkami luḷḷat tārvanaṉ kūkkat
taṇṭarum peyariya paṇṭiyōr nūlvaḻip
paṉṉutol kāppiya naṉṉū leṉavum
aintira pāṇi ṉīya meṉavum			60
camaraca pāṭiya cittānta meṉavum
vaḷampā ṭilāta iḷampū raṇamum
meyccolāṟ paḻiccu nacciṉārk kiṉiyamum
vāṉōr māṇpami cēṉā variyamum
caṅkara namacci vāyamu meṉṟivi			65
1ainturi yuntokut tiṉpuṟa vuṇmiyiṉ
pūraṇa virutti yeṉavum peyarpuṉin
tinnū liyaṟṟi yeḻutā veḻuttiṟ
paṉṉū ṉeṟiyiṟ patittu nalkiṉaṉ;
vaḷinīrk karuṅkaṭaṉ maṟitiri koḻikkum	70
uḷivuṟīip paṇila muyirttata nittilam
karuṅkōṭ ṭuppuṉṉi naṟuṅkē ḻarumpar
kurukiṉa muyirkkum parūumaṇi yoṇkaru
iḵtaḵteṉā vayirppuṟavayiṉ ṟorunto kūum
naṟivār cōlip piṟiyā ṟeṉunakar		75
vatitaru cōḻa vēḷāḷa marapiṟ
patitaru tāṇṭava rāyavēḷ payanta
nāṭa varucuvi cēṭatic tānti
ceṅkuvaḷi yantār taṅkumaṇi mārpaṉ
nāma vēlip puvi nayakkum			80
cāmu vēleṉun takimi yōṉē.
kucēlōpākkiyāṉam
vallūr tēvarāja piḷḷiyavarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya kucēlō pākkiyāṉattiṟkut tiricirapuram makāvittuvāṉ mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya ciṟappuppāyiram.
nērici āciriyappā
	pūmali kuḷirteṇ puṉaṟperun taṭattuk
kaviyaṭic ceṅkaṭ kañalpiṇar maruppuk
kaṭamaruḷ ceruttaṟ kavaritaḷi parintu
puḻaṟkā lāmpaṟ pūnaṉi micivāṉ
ṟōṟoṭu nāṭpiñ cūṭṭiṉa miriya			5
maṭacceḻu nāri vayireṉap piḷiṟak
kayaliṉa maṭavār kaṇṇiṟ piṟaḻac
calañcalap puñcan taḷipuk koḷippa
muṭṭāṭ ṭāmari moyttaṭi kiṭakku
mañciṟit tumpiyakaṉkulip parappiṟ		10
ṟēṅkamaḻ poṅkarc ceṟintiṟi koḷḷa
māṅkaṉik koḷḷi varampila vutirak
kuṟuṅkaḻut tarampik kūṉkuli muṟiya
muṭpuṟap pākaṉ mutirkaṉi kiḻiyac
ceṟipacuṅ kantic ceḻumpaḻañ citaṟap
poliyilāṅ kalimup puṭikkāy vīḻa
veḻupumīt takaṭṭakaṭ ṭiḷavarāl pāyak
karuṅkaṭaṟ pāyuṅ kantara nikarppak
kataḻiviṟ pāyntu kalakkupuḻi tantu
katiriḷam pacumpuṟ kaṟikkak kallā			20
maḻaviḷaṅ kaṉṟip paṭarntuvārn toḻukap
poḻipāl perukip puṇartirit tīmpāl
āḻiko lōveṉ ṟamararu mayirppa
maṭiyuṭit tōṭum variyāp peruvaḷa
nallū reṉṉum vallū ratipaṉ			25
pārkaru ṇīppap payilkula meṉṉuñ
cīrkaru ṇīkar ceḻuṅkula cikāmaṇi
miṉṉap pēntiya vēṇiyaṟ kaṉpuṟu
maṉṉap pēntiraṉ varattiṉi lutittōṉ
imiyar taruvu mimiyāc curapiyuṅ		30
kamañcūṉ mukiluṅ katirkāṉ maṇiyum 
aruniti yiraṇṭu maḻukkā ṟaṭiyap
peruṅkoṭi yevarkkun taruṅkara vaḷḷal
oḻukkamuñ cīlamum viḻuppeṟu tavamu
māṉā vaṉpu māṉamum poṟiyu			35
nāṭoṟu nāṭoṟu nalampeṟa vaḷarppōṉ
nīṉiṟañ cērā neṭumāl pōlvāṉ
nuṇṇaṟi vuṭiyōr nūṟporuḷ virumpun
taṇṇaḷi yirāma cāmik kuricil
taṉṉuṭi yarumpeṟaṟ ṟamaya ṉākiya		40
taḻipukaḻ vīrāc cāmi makipaṉ
uñaṟṟiya tavattā lutayañ ceyta
tēva rācac cevvē ḷeṉpāṉ
naṟkuṇam poṟumi ñāṉa voḻukkaṅ
kuṟiyā taminta niṟivu pūṇṭu			45
tolkāp piyamutaṟ ṟōṉṟilak kaṇamu
muṉṉumiṅ kāppiya mutalilak kiyamum
paṟpala camaya raṟpuṟu nūlum
maṟṟuḷa kalikaḷuṅ kaṟṟiṉi tuṇarntu
mikuvaṭa nūluḷum vēṇṭiya terintu		50
cōtiṭa nūlun turicaṟa vōrntu
piṟaṅkuṟu maṟiviṟ periyō ṉāki
vaṇpukaḻk kucēla mātavaṉ caritiyit
tamiḻiṟ pāṭat tāṉaḵ taṟintu
taṉṉavik kaḷattu maṉṉuṟa vaḻittut		55
tokkaḷa villār cūḻa viruppa
viritturik  keṉṟu viruppiṟ kēṭṭuk
kavitoṟuṅ kiṭakkuṅ kaṟpaṉi viyappuñ
colluṭi māṇpun tokuporuḷ māṇpum
maṟṟuḷa ciṟappum vakippaṭa vārāyn		60
taṟputa maṟputa meṉṟaka makiḻntu
virikaṭal cūḻpuvi viḷaṅkap
parivuṭa ṉacciṟ patippit taṉaṉē.
	ērāruñ cakāttamā yiratteḻu nūṟ ṟeḻupattiraṇṭi
	ṉikaḻcumiya nallāṇṭu taṉut tiṅkaḷ
vārāruṅ mirupattu nāṉkānāḷ paritivāra
	moṉpāṉ ṟiticōti ciṅkavilak kiṉattiṟ
pērāruṅ kucēlamuṉi taṉatucarit tirattip
	peṭpiṉiṉi taruntamiḻi ṉiyalceṟiyap pāṭit
tārārum puyattēva rācavaḷḷa lāṉ ṟōrtaḻit
	tuvakipūppa araṅkēṟ ṟiṉaṉuṇ makiḻntē.
niṭata uri
tiruttaṇiki caravaṇap perumāḷiyarum avar makaṉār kantacuvāmi aiyarum niṭatattukku uri eḻutiṉārkaḷ. anta nūl cupakirutu varucam vikāci mātam kalvi viḷakka accuk kūṭattil acciṟpatip pikkappaṭṭatu. annūlukku, “yūṉivar siṭṭi yeṉṉum cakala kalā cāstira cālit tamiḻt talimip pulavarākiya” maḻavi makāliṅkiyar aḷitta ciṟappup pāyiram itu:
	“matikkaṇa moruṅku kiṭanteṉa vayaṅkuñ
 cilliya viṟāliṉ ṟiraḷiṉi vāṉkaḻi
 kōṭukoṇ ṭuḻutiṭak koṭṭiya centēṉ
 aruvika ṭuyalvaru mamiti pūmātiṉ
 ṟaṭamulic cemmaṇit tāḻvaṭa mēykku		5
 mimayamāl variyi ṉiruntavat teḻunta
 marakatak komparōr māvuṟi mutalit
 taḻīiyeṇ ṇāṉkaṟan tappā tiyaṟṟuṅ
 kacciyiṉ kīḻpāṟ kaṭaṟkōṭ ṭirunta
 miyiliyū rakatti ṉayiliyōr karaṅkoṇ	10
 ṭaṭaṟcū ruyirkuṭit tamararip puranta
 kantaṉē ñāṉacam pantaṉāk kāḻiyi
 ṉavatarit tamaṇkaḷi yaṉittum vērpōkkic
 civap piṅkūḻ taḻivit tataṉpiṉ
 kaṭattuṟu mattiyik kaṇṭu maṭṭiṭṭa		15
 puṉṉiyaṅ kāṉa leṉappukal patikan
 taṉṉāṉ muṉṉart takicā luruvam
 peṟṟapūm pāvi yuṟṟanaṟ kulattōṉ
 kuṟippaṟin tīkuva tuttamak koṭiyaṉ
 ṟivaṉāṟ poruṇamak keytu meṉṟuḷḷat	20
 tuḷḷalu miṉṟiyōṉ viḷḷaru makiḻcci
 pūttiṭa vaḷittal poṟpuṟu mutaṟkoṭi
 yāṉeṉak koṇṭa varumicēr vaḷḷal
 ayalavar naṇpiṉa rarikaḷā mūvaki
 maṟṟiyark kuḷanaṇ puṟṟavōt takiyē		25
 yivarkkuḷa teṉacco lētamil kuṇattōṉ
 vēntartam viṭaṅka mēluyart teṭuppap
 pōnta kāraṇap poṟpuṟu mēḻik
 koṭiyiṉaṉ ṟaṉpāṟ kuṟiyiran torukāṉ
 miṭayaṉyā ṉeṉṟu viḷampuvōr nāvā		30
 ṉaḷakiyāṉ miṭiyaṉeṉ ṟaṟintiṭa ceyvōṉ
 ēṉṟanaṟ kuṇaṅkaḷuk keṭuttuk kāṭṭu
 māṉṟa pallō rāyateṟ ṟiṉukkiv
 voruvaṉē cālu mutāraṇa meṉṉa
 vayaṉmaṉa muvappuṟ ṟamikka vantō		35
 ṉiṉaṅko ṭōrporuḷ kavarntiṭutaṉ māliya teṉap
 puyattuṟu kuvaḷiyiṟ poṉṉeṉap pitir cuṇaṅ
 kavirmulik kaṉṉiya rariviḻit tiraḷiṉik
 kavarvuṟu cantaṅ kaṉintiṭu vilvēḷ
 eṉuñciṅ kāravēn tiṉpuṟak kaḷikūrn		40
 tutati cuvaṟṟu muṭampiṭi paritta
 mīṉavar kulattuti vīra rāma
 ṉiyaṟṟiya niṭata meṉunaṟ kāppiya
 mēvar māṭṭu miyintu vaḻaṅkaliṟ
 coṟporuṭ piḻikaḷ paṟpala vuṟṟatu		45
 kaṟṟuval lārumak kavalvaḻūuk kaṭintu
 muṭipōrn tataṉporuḷ moḻiyalō ruriyu
 miṉṟitu kāṟu mātali ṉērpeṟa
 vēynta vōruri yiyaṟṟuka veṉṉa
 alamaru kaṉma makaṟṟiṭun taṇikit		50
 talamvaḷar vīra civamā kēcaṉ
 centamiḻk kaṭaloru cēraneñ caṭakki
 yeṇṭicip parappa reṅkaṇum peytu
 maṉpati yuṇarcciyi vaḷarkkuṅ koṇṭa
 livaṉkū riyamati yēkuṟu matariṉuṭ		55
 cēṭaṉār parumati celaṟpāṟ ṟaṉṟeṉa
 yāva ruḷḷattu maracuvīṟ ṟirunta
 pērpeṟuñ caravaṇap porumā ḷiyaṉ
 vaṭamoḻi taṉṉiṉ māṇpuṟ ṟuyarnta
 vitaṉmuta ṉūṟku miyipuṟa nāṭi			60
 iyalpu mōṉi yetuki paṟṟi
 vīṟṟuṟu palavām vikaṟpamuṅ karutit
 tūkkut tōṟun tukaḷaṟu maṇivirit
 titaṉuk koppō vennū luriyumeṉ
 ṟiruntamiḻp pulava reṇṇilar palakāṟ		65
 pakarntiṭak kikkiḷip paṭalamāṭ ṭuñaṟṟiṉa
 ṉataṉpiṉ ṉavaṉṟavat tārntakāṉ muḷiyeṉuṅ
 kanta cuvāmiyāñ centamiḻ nāvalaṉ
 pulamicēr cilartam pōtaru mutaviyiṉ
 maṟṟiya paṭalamun teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkac		70
 ceytamaruṅ kīrtti yeytiṉaṉ makiḻntē.
ilakkaṇa viṉāviṭi
1828-ām āṇṭu ceṉṉik kalvic caṅkattil acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭa tāṇṭavarāya mutuliyār ceyta “ilakkaṇa viṉāviṭi” eṉṉum nūliṉ pāyiram:
“ceṉṉik kalvic caṅkattatiparākiya kaṉam poruntiyā pōrṭutturi yavarkaḷuttaraviṉāl cālīvākaṉa caka āṇṭu 1746-kku mēṟcellā niṉṟa pārttiva āṇṭukkuc cariyāṉa kiristu 
1825-ām āṇṭu ilakkaṇa viṉāviṭi ceytu muṟṟuppeṟṟatu.
	perunūluṭ pukalaṟiyāp piḷḷikaṇ
	ṇeḷituṇarap pēcu vāyō
rurinūl viṉāviṭiyāt tamiḻiyaleṉ
	ṟaruṭkuṇattā ṉuṇarviṉ mikkāṉ
arunūlkaḷ viḷaṅku ceṉṉic caṅkattut
	talivaṉ kiḷārk katipaṉ kūṟa
virinūlāy karuviyeṉat tokiyuṟattāṇ
	ṭavarāyaṉ viḷampi ṉāṉāl.
	kuṟṟamuṅ kuṇamāk koḷumaṟiña rakkoḷki
maṟṟeṉ pāliṉum vaḻāreṉpa taṟintayāṉ vaḻātu
kaṟṟanā valaricciṉ ṉūṉum mavikkaḷattuc
coṟṟiṭark kañci niṟpavō mūtakkīr collīr.
kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkam
	ilakkaṇa miṉṉatiḵteṉa yārkkum
aḷakka voṇṇātata ṉaṭiparavutum
ilakkaṇa viṉāviṭi viḷakka muṟaṟporuṭṭē
naṉṉūṟ kāṇṭikiyuri
iyaṟṟamiḻāciriyar irāmānuca kavirāyar 1846-il naṉṉūlukku viruttiyuri eḻuti acciṭṭārkaḷ. avvirutti yuriyiṉ toṭakkattil kīḻkkaṇṭa ceyyuḷi iyaṟṟiyamit tārkaḷ. “malartaliyulakiṉ malkiruḷ akala” eṉa varum naṉṉūl ciṟappup pāyirat tiṉkīḻ icceyyuḷ vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tēkikar cēvaṭi maṉakku
mācaṟu maṇimuṭi yeṉakku.
	kaṭalpuṭi cūḻtarūuṅ kāciṉi yataṉiṭi
vaṭaticik kaṇmarīi vaḷaṉelāñ curantu
muṉimuṉa naṭantuṟūu mūṅkiliṟ paṭarntu

ṟiyaṉṟavi yiyaṟṟu minnūluḷ
ittalic cūttiram yātuṇarttiṟṟōveṉiṉ, ciṟappup pāyirat tīlakkaṇamāka vakuttōtappaṭṭa, nūl ceytōṉ peyar mutaliya patiṉoṉṟiyu muṇarttiṟṟeṉka.
vivakāra cāra caṅkirakam
maturik kantacāmip pulavar ceṉṉik kalvic caṅkattil iruntavar. vivakāra cāra caṅkirakam eṉṉum nūli vaṭamoḻi yiliruntu tamiḻil eḻutiṉār. itu 1894-ām āṇṭu acciṭap paṭṭatu. itaṉ ciṟappup pāyiram itu:
	tiruvaruṇ mūrtti yōriñ ceyaliṉaṉ ṟēvar tēvā
moruvaṉal lirutā ḷaṉpi ṉuḷaṅkoṭu paṇintu mēṉmi
taruviva kāra cāra caṅkiraka meṉṉu nāma
maruviya tarumanūli vakutturi ceytu naṉṟē.
------
	ulakiṉuṟu narar palarum vivakāra meḷituṇara
	vurinaṭiyi ṉāṟṟa rumanū
loṉṟuñaṟ ṟutiyeṉṟu nampiṉar tamikkākku
	muttamaṉ citti yaṉcī
rilakuceṉ ṉittiṟaṟ caṅkat tamiḻttalimi
	yēṟṟaric cārṭṭuk kiḷārk
kēntal cola viṅkilī cīriraṇṭāñ cārcci
	yiṟinīti yaracu ceyunā
ḷalakukoṇ ṭeṇcāli vākaṉa cakāttamō
	rāyirat teḻunūṟ ṟōṭē
ḻāṟī riraṇṭiṉaṉ ṟōṅkumaṉ ṟōmaṉṉa
	ṉāḷumati kārat tiṉiṟ
calamiṉ maṉu vātimiru tikaḷōrntu vivakāra
	cāracaṅ kiraka meṉunūṟ
ṟaṉṉip purintaṉaṉ ṟeṉṉaṉmatu rikkanta
	cāminā tappulavaṉē.
arāpik katiyiṉ ciṟappu
1876-ām āṇṭu arāpik kati tamiḻil moḻi peyark kappaṭṭu acciṭappaṭṭatu. itaṉi amarampēṭu aṇṇācāmi mutaliyār moḻipeyarttār. innūl ceṉṉi cintātirip pēṭṭip pirapākara accuk kūṭattil acciṟ patippikkappaṭṭatu. itaṟkut taraṅkāpuram caṇmukak kavirāyar eḻutiya “katiyiṉ ciṟappu” eṉṉum muṉṉuriyik kīḻē tarukiṟēṉ.
“kaṭavuḷā lērppaṭuttappaṭṭa kaṭalcūḻnta kāciṉiyiṟ cintutēca mutalākiya namatintu tēcattilum munta mutaṟ ṟoṭaṅki antamāka vantirukkiṉṟa vintiyāṉa carittiraṅka ḷellāvaṟṟilum iṉimi paṟṟi mutaṉmiyuṟṟu makimi coṟṟa perumi peṟṟa iracakkiyap poruḷi vitvattiṟattiṉ mikutta piracitta carittira vicitra katittiṟa vitteṉa yukti vitittu viḷittaruḷ puttiyiṉ caktiporunti yorutti irāvāṟ kūṟiya aṟāpik katiyiṉi muṉṉōr mutaliya muttiya peṟṟōrkaḷ toṉṉūl naṉṉūl tutinūl vitinūl eṉṉūlākavu miyaṟṟiya tamiḻā luṟuttamākat tirutta māyc ceytu nāvalarumpukaḻk kāvalarum makiḻa icci mēlōṅka vacciṭṭamiyāl (ikkati yiṉ perumiyik kāṭṭumiṭattu) jekamelām purakkuñ cintu nāṭṭiṟivaṉ tiṉamoru vivākañcey tutayattiṟ koṉṟu viṭavēṇṭumeṉṟu kuṟittu naṭattiya karuttik kuṟittu kaṇkaḷ nīrtatumpap peṇkaḷi yiḻantōr peruntuyar kaḷintu mantiri koṇṭa maruṭciyi māṟṟi āyiratteṭṭirā vakaṟṟi yaṉpaṉōṭē yiruttiṉpamuṟṟiḷiya mātuṭaṉ ṣayarasāṭ ṭeṉpavaḷ cattiyāl mūṉṟu puttirap pēṟu peṟṟu vāḻntiruntāḷ.
puttiyāl yukti pukaṉṟa vikkatiyip pattiyāl vācikkum pāvalarkaḷ mutaliya maṟṟevarkaṭkum vācaka tāṭṭiyum vākkiṉa takalamum vacaṉavucitamum varicik kiramamum mariyātip perukkamum vaṇpatap poruḷum eḻuvāy payaṉili ici canti niliyum pirittalum virittalum coṟpayaṉ puṇarttalun tokuttalum vakuttalum aṟivuniṟivum apalakkuṟivum neṟinili nītiyum aṟampoṟi prakyātiyum capikkup yōkamum taṉakku viyōkamum tantira yuktiyum mantiraccaktiyum tiyalār cōrvum miyalāṟ cōrvum kaṭavuḷar pattiyum aṭiyalar veṟṟiyum kalviyiṉūkkamuñ cevviyiṉōkkamum nāyakaṉēcamum nāyaki pācamum tantitāyp pēṇalum taṉ pantu kāṇalum tāmpukaḻ pūṇalum tamikkaṇṭōr nāṇalum nūtaṉa viruntum ātaṉattiruntum tirikaṭalōṭiyun tiraviyan tēṭiyum cērppaṉa cērttum pārppaṉa pārttum cevippulaṉ makiḻntum cintiyuṭ kaḷittum iṉṉamuḷḷa naṉmikaḷ yāviyum eḷitiṟ perun teḷivittaru miṉ pakkati yiḵtaṉṟiyum.
periya vayatiṉiyuṭiya mutiyōrkaḷum ārōkkiya stāṉañ ceytavarkaḷum viyātikkārar mutaliya melivuḷ ḷōrum taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ cukaṅkaḷukkāka paktisrat tiyiṉāl camaya campiratāya cāstira nūlkaḷi ārāycci ceyyavāvatu kēṭkavāvatu ēṟpaṭum pōtu cilarukku uṟakkamum cōmpalum veṟuppum ōyvu muṇṭākiṟa tuṇṭu. appērppaṭṭa kālattil cokkaṭṭāṉ. cōḻi caturaṅkam kāyitam āṭat toṭaṅku vārkaḷ. antac camayatti lippērppaṭṭa katiyiṉip paṭikkavuṅ kēṭkavum paṭcam vippārkaḷākil intac cutāracamāṉatu kātiṉ vaḻiyē kacappillāma ṉuḻintu tēkapīṭiyi nīkki, tirēkac cōmpal veṟuppu oṟuppu uṟakka mutaliyatip pōkki ākka muṇṭākkuvatu mallāmal ikkatiyik kēṭpavarkaḷ, mēṟkati yeppaṭiyō; atik kēṭkavēṇu meṉṉum ēkkamēyaṉṟi tūkka mirātu. vēṟu viṣayaṅkaḷil nōkkamvarātu. vitaraṇi yuṭiya puttimāṉkaḷukku yukati nuṭpam mallātaṉa vaṟṟit tarātu. ittiram vāynta tikcutic ciṟappē.
vintit tamiḻāl viritturi ceyta intustāṉ carittira mākiya arāpikkatiyi, cintātirippēṭṭi ci. nārāyaṇa cāmi mutaliyārāṟ ṟamatu pirapākaravaccuk kūṭattiṟa patippikkappaṭṭatu. yuva āṇṭu māci mātam.

(eḻucīrk kaḻineṭilaṭi yāciriya viruttam)
	pērpeṟu marāpip pāṣiyiṟ piṟantu
	peruntuṟi mukamelāñ ciṟantu
cīrpeṟum pāṣān tiraṅkaḷū ṭuḻaṉṟu
	tiruntiya katimukac celvi
kārpeṟu mamaram pēṭṭil vāḻaṇṇāc
	cāmitaṉ karuttiṉāṟ porunti
ēṟpeṟun tamiḻāl alaṅkarit teḻuntāḷ
	yāvaru miṉapuṟat poruṭṭē.










19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil accāṉa 
ilakkiya nūlkaḷ
19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē accukkūṭam vikkum urimi nammavarukkuk kiṭittapōtu paḻiya, putiya nūlkaḷ accup puttakaṅkaḷāka veḷivantaṉa. avi kaṇakkilaṭaṅkā. paḻiya ilakkiya nūlkaḷ mutal mutalāka ēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyiliruntu accup puttakamāka veḷivantavaṟṟi iṅkuk kāṭṭukiṟēṉ. ituvum muḻuvivarap paṭṭiyal aṉṟu. itil pala nūlkaḷ viṭupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. terintavaṟṟil cilavaṟṟi maṭṭum kīḻē tarukiṟēṉ.

19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil 
urinaṭi nūlkaḷ
19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, nammavarum acciyantiram vippataṟku vāyppu ēṟpaṭṭa piṟaku tamiḻil urinaṭi nūlkaḷ pala veḷi vantaṉa. pāṭacālip putta kaṅkaḷākavum, camaya nūlkaḷākavum, katikaḷākavum, vēṟu poruḷ paṟṟiyum pala uri naṭi nūlkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil accup puttakaṅkaḷāka veḷivantaṉa. annūlkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum tokuttup paṭṭiyalāka amippatu iyalātu. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil accup puttakamāka veḷivanta uri naṭi nūlkaḷil cilavaṟṟi maṭṭum iṅkup paṭṭiyalākat tarukiṟēṉ. itu muḻu vivarap paṭṭiyal aṉṟu eṉpatik karutavēṇṭukiṟēṉ.

19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil 
nāṭaka nūlkaḷ
19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil nāṭaka nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭu acciṭap paṭṭaṉa. oṉṟiraṇṭu paḻaya nāṭaka nūlkaḷum acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. inta nāṭaka nūlkaḷil icip pāṭṭukaḷ atikamākavum vacaṉaṅkaḷ kuṟivākavum amintiruntaṉa. ivi paḻaya nāṭaka ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷiṉ muṟippaṭi illāmalum mēlnāṭṭu muṟippaṭi illāmalum putumuṟiyāka amintiruntaṉa. cilappatikāram mutaliya paḻaya nūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭukiṟa nāṭaka muṟippaṭi illāmalum ṣēkspiyar nāṭakaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa mēlnāṭṭu muṟippaṭi illāmalum inta nāṭaka nūlkaḷ putu muṟiyāka amintiruntaṉa. ivi perumpālum purāṇak katikaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu iyaṟṟappaṭṭavi. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil inta nāṭaka nūlkaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu iyaṟṟap paṭṭavi. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil inta nāṭaka nūlkaḷi atikamākap pōṟṟiṉārkaḷ. tamiḻnāṭṭil maṭṭum allāmal, maliyāḷa nāṭṭilum it tamiḻ nāṭaka nūlkaḷ celvākkup peṟṟiruntaṉa. cila tamiḻ nāṭaka nūlkaḷ maliyāḷa eḻuttil acciṭappaṭṭu viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. cāvittiri nāṭakam maliyāḷa eḻuttil pālakkāṭṭil 1890-ām āṇṭu acciṭappaṭṭatu. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil pēr peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya “catāram” eṉṉum nāṭaka nūli “satāram” eṉṉum peyaril maliyāḷa nāṭṭil acciṭṭārkaḷ. tamiḻ nāṭaka nūlkaḷ kēraḷa nāṭṭil pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. hariccantiraṉ, kōvalaṉ carittiram. nallataṅkāḷ, kaṇṇaki, vaḷḷi tirumaṇam mutaliya tamiḻ nāṭaka nūlkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil kēraḷattil ciṟappup peṟṟiruntaṉa.
inta nāṭaka nūlkaḷil, pāṭṭukaḷ atikam. “cākappōkum taruvāyilum pāṭṭu, cettuk kiṭakkum pōtum pāṭṭu, aḻumpōtum pāṭṭu, enta entac cantarppattilum pāṭṭu” eṉṟu inta nāṭakaṅ kaḷippaṟṟi oruvar eḻutukiṟār. ām. inta nāṭakaṅkaḷ toṭakkam mutal muṭivu variyil pāṭṭākavē iruntaṉa. iṭiyiṭiyē cila vacaṉaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. inta vacaṉaṅkaḷum kaṭṭiyakkāraṉ kūṟṟāka iruntaṉavē yallāmal, nāṭakap pāttiraṅkaḷiṉ uriyāṭalkaḷāka alla. āṉāl, akkālattilē inta nāṭakaṅkaḷukku nāṭṭilē atika celvākkiruntatu.
eṉṉuṭiya iḷamik kālattilē, eṉṉuṭiya illattil irunta nūl niliyattil inta nāṭaka nūlkaḷil palavaṟṟip pārkkavum paṭikkavum nāṉ vāyppup peṟṟiruntēṉ. ceṉṟa 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil acciṭappaṭṭa nāṭaka nūlkaḷiṉ peyariyum, āciriyar peyariyum acciṭṭavar peyariyum nāṉ aṟinta variyil kīḻē tarukiṟēṉ. itu muḻuppaṭṭiyalāka irukka muṭiyātu.
āṇṭu	nūliṉ peyar		patippāciriyar			āciriyar peyar		peyar.

mayili cīṉi vēṅkaṭacāmi 
āyvuk kaḷañciyam
tokuti varicikaḷ
tokuti - 1	:	paṇṭit tamiḻaka varalāṟu:  
		cērar - cōḻar - pāṇṭiyar
		ittokutiyil caṅka kāla tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷ kuṟitta ilakkiyam maṟṟum āvaṇaṅkaḷ cārnta varalāṟṟu āyvukaḷ iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ipporuḷil  taṉi nūlāka ippōtutāṉ tokukkappaṭukiṉṟatu. palvēṟu nūlkaḷil iṭampeṟṟavi iṅku orucēra uḷḷaṉa. 
tokuti - 2	:	paṇṭit tamiḻaka varalāṟu:  
koṅku nāṭu - pāṇṭiyar - pallavar -  ilaṅki varalāṟu
		paṇṭit tamiḻakattil koṅku pakuti taṉit taṉmi yōṭu viḷaṅkiya pakutiyākum. mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmi avarkaḷ koṅku pakutikaḷ kuṟittu ceyta āyvukaḷ iṅku tokukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pallava maṉṉarkaḷ paṟṟit taṉittaṉiyāṉa nūlkaḷi mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ eḻutiṉār. ivaṟṟiliruntu maṉṉarkaḷ kuṟittu varalāṟukaḷ maṭṭum  ittokutiyil tokukkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. pāṇṭiyarkaḷ kuṟitta takavalkaḷum ittokutiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. ilaṅki tamiḻar varalāṟu kuṟittup pala iṭaṅkaḷil mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷavi ittokutiyil iṇikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
tokuti - 3	:	paṇṭit tamiḻaka varalāṟu: 
kaḷappirar - tuḷu nāṭu
		iruṇṭa kālam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa varalāṟṟil oḷi pāycciya āyvu kaḷappirar paṟṟiya āyvu ākum. iṉṟiya karnāṭakap pakutiyiluḷḷa tuḷu nāṭu paṟṟiyum ivar eḻutiyuḷḷār. ivviraṇṭu nūlkaḷum it tokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tokuti - 4	:	paṇṭit tamiḻakam:
vaṇikam - nakaraṅkaḷ maṟṟum  paṇpāṭu
		mayili cīṉi palvēṟu taruṇaṅkaḷil eḻutiya paṇṭit tamiḻarkaḷiṉ vaṇikam, paṇṭit tamiḻaka nakaraṅkaḷ maṟṟum palvēṟu paṇpāṭṭuc ceytikaḷ ittokutiyil tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tokuti - 5	:	paṇṭit tamiḻakam: 
āvaṇam - pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ - naṭukaṟkaḷ
		tamiḻarkaḷiṉ tolleḻuttiyal toṭarpāṉa āyvukaḷ tamiḻil mikakkuṟivē. kaḷāyvu mūlam mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟinta pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ maṟṟum naṭukaṟkaḷ toṭarpāṉa āyvukaḷ ittokutiyil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tokuti - 6	:	paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ:  
kālārāycci  - ilakkiya ārāycci
		paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ kālam paṟṟiya palvēṟu muraṇpaṭṭa āyvukaḷ tamiḻil nikaḻntuḷḷaṉa. mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ tamatu kaṇṇōṭṭattil tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kuṟittuc ceytu kāla āyvukaḷ it tokutiyil tokukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻk kāppiyaṅ kaḷ maṟṟum pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kuṟittu mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ ceyta āyvukaḷum it tokutiyil iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tokuti - 7	:	tamiḻakac camayaṅkaḷ: 
camaṇam
		‘camaṇamum tamiḻum’ eṉṟa poruḷil mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ eḻutiya nūl ittokutiyil iṭampeṟu kiṉṟatu. camaṇam kuṟittu ivar eḻutiya vēṟu pala kaṭṭurikaḷ it tokutiyil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
tokuti - 8	:	tamiḻakac camayaṅkaḷ:
puttam
		‘puttamum tamiḻum’ eṉṉum poruḷil ivar eḻutiya āyvukaḷ ittokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṉṟaṉa. puttam toṭarpāka palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil mayili cīṉi eḻutiya kaṭṭurikaḷ aṉittum ittokuti yil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tokuti - 9	:	tamiḻil camayam: 
kutamaputtariṉ vāḻkki
		puttariṉ varalāṟu putta jātakak katikaḷi aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvakiyil mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ eḻutiya puttariṉ vāḻkki varalāṟu it tokutiyil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷatu. 
tokuti - 10	:	tamiḻil camayam:
puttak katikaḷ - icivāṇar katikaḷ
		1940 mutal puttak katikaḷi tamiḻil moḻi peyarttavar mayili cīṉi. puttak kalivāṇarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki varalāṟṟiyum ivar tokuttuḷḷār. iv viraṇṭu nūlkaḷum ittokutiyil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. 
tokuti - 11	:	tamiḻil camayam:
putta jātakak katikaḷ
		putta jātakak katikaḷ tamiḻil palarāl moḻipeyarkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ ceytuḷḷa putta jātakak katikaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppu ittokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṟatu.
tokuti - 12	:	tamiḻakak kali varalāṟu:
ciṟpam - kōyil
		mayili cīṉi tamiḻaka ciṟpaṅkaḷ maṟṟum kōyilkaḷ kuṟittu virivāṉa āyvukaḷi mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷār. palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷa avvāyvu kaḷi ittokutiyil orucērat tokuttuḷḷōm.
tokuti - 13	:	tamiḻaka kali varalāṟu: 
		ici - ōviyam - aṇikalaṉkaḷ
		tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paṇmarapu kuṟittum tamiḻ nāṭṭu ōviyam kuṟittum virivāṉa āyvi ivar mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷār. ivvāyvukaḷ aṉittum ittokutiyil tokukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉ kuṟittu mayili cīṉi. eḻutiyuḷḷa āyvukaḷum ittokutiyil iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tokuti - 14	:	tamiḻaka āvaṇaṅkaḷ: 
cācaṉac ceyyuḷ - ceppēṭukaḷ - kalveṭṭukaḷ
		kalveṭṭukkaḷil  ceyyuḷkaḷ mikutiyāka eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi aṉittiyum tokuttu cācaṉac ceyyuḷ mañcari eṉṟa oru nūli mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭārkaḷ. tamiḻaka varalāṟu toṭarpāka ceppēṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭum virivāṉa takavalkaḷ paṟṟi ivar āyvu ceytuḷḷār. tamiḻil uḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷ toṭarpākavum mayili cīṉi avarkaḷuṭiya āyvukaḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavi. ivi aṉittum ittokutiyil iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
tokuti - 15	:	tamiḻaka āvaṇaṅkaḷ: 
maṟintupōṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ
		maṟintupōṉa tamiḻnūlkaḷ eṉṉum peyaril mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ nūl oṉṟi eḻutiyuḷḷār. cumār 250 maṟintu pōṉa nūlkaḷ paṟṟiya virivāṉa takavalkaḷi innūlil avar tokuttuḷḷār. annūl ittokutiyil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tokuti - 16	:	tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu: 
pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭu
		pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭu tamiḻ ilakkiyam eṉṉum peyaril mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ eḻutiya nūl ittokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṟatu. ipporuḷ kuṟittu tamiḻil uḷḷa ariya nūl ituveṉṟu kūṟumuṭiyum.
tokuti - 17	:	tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu: 
kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum
		airōppiya iyēcu capikaḷ mūlamāka tamiḻakattiṟku varukippurinta pātiriyārkaḷ tamiḻukku ceyta toṇṭu aḷaviṭaṟpālatu. ippaṇikaḷ aṉitti yum ivar tokuttuḷḷār; maṟṟum tamiḻil accuk kalimūlam uruvāṉa palvēṟu putiya viḷivukaḷ kuṟittum mayili cīṉi eḻutiyuḷḷār. ivi aṉittum ittokutiyil tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tokuti - 18	:	tamiḻiyal āyvu: 
collāyvukaḷ - vāḻkki varalāṟu
		palvēṟu taruṇaṅkaḷil paṇpāṭṭuc ceytikaḷukkut taravāka coṟkaḷ amivatu kuṟittu mayili cīṉi avarkaḷ āyvu ceytuḷḷār. ivvāyvukaḷ it tokutiyil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pala aṟiñarkaḷil vāḻkki varalāṟu kuṟittum ivar eḻutiyuḷḷār ac ceytikaḷum ittokutiyil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tokuti - 19	:	patippu, moḻipeyarppu, uri: 
nēminātam - nantikalampakam -  piṟa
		nēminātam, nantikalampakam ākiyavaṟṟi ivar patippittuḷḷār. ‘mattavilācam’ eṉṉum camaskiruta nāṭaka nūli moḻipeyarttuḷḷār. innūlkaḷ it tokutiyil iṇikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mayili nēmi nātar patikam eṉṟa oru nūliyum ivar patippit tuḷḷār. annūl ittokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṟatu. uṇavu muṟikaḷ kuṟittu ivar eḻutiya uḷḷa nūlum ittokutiyil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tokuti - 20	:	patippu: 
maṉōṉmaṇiyam nāṭakam
		maṉōṉmaṇiyam cuntarappiḷḷi eḻutiya  maṉōṉ maṇiya nāṭakatti mayili cīṉi patippittuḷḷār. in nāṭakam kuṟitta virivāṉa āyvuriyiyum innūlil ceytuḷḷār. innūl ittokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṟatu. 
vī. aracu


kuṟippukaḷ

aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	pārata nāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vanta eṇvaṭivaṅkaḷip pāracīkar eṭuttu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ inta eṇkaḷukku ‘intu’ eṇkaḷ eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. piṟaku inta eṇkaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷip pāracīkariṭa miruntu arāpiyar eṭuttuk koṇṭu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ. arāpiyar inta eṇkaḷukkup “pāracīka eṇkaḷ” eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. piṟaku arāpiyariṭamiruntuinta eṇkaḷi airōppayir eṭuttuk koṇṭu vaḻaṅkiya pōtu itaṟku “arapi” eṇkaḷ eṉṟu peyariṭṭārkaḷ.
2.	Leonardo Fibonacci.
3.	intak kūṭṭal kaḻittal kuṟikaḷ airōppiya varttakarkaḷāl mutaṉ mulāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. varttakarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ aṉuppum carakku mūṭṭikaḷiṉ mēl intak kuṟikaḷiyiṭṭārkaḷ. + intak kuṟi, mūṭṭiyil carakkukaḷ atikamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatit terivikkavum, - intak kuṟi, mūṭṭiyil carakkukaḷ kuṟivāka uḷḷaṉa eṉpatit terivikkavum carakku mūṭṭikaḷiṉ mēl viyāpārikaḷāl kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. piṟaku, kaṇita nūlilē kūṭṭal, kaḻittal kuṟiyākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. (cīṉi. vē.)
4.	pācciyam = vakukkappaṭum eṇ.
5.	pācakam = vakukkum eṇ
6.	Manual of the Administration of the Madras Presidency. Vol II. 1885.

aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Its Prose - Style is yet in a forming state, and will well repay the labour of accurate scholars in moulding properly. Many Natives, who write poetry readily, cannot write a page of correct prose.
- Preface. winslow’s Tamil - English Dictionary. 1862.
2.	Nearly the whole of Tamil Literature, including works on medicine, Arithmetic, grammar and even Dictionaries, is in poetry but the exception of the commentaries on poetical works. Prose composition may almost be sid to owe  its origin to European influence.
	- Preface. Classified catalogue of Tamil Printed Books. by John Murdoch. Madras - 1865.
3.	* Grammars, Dictionaries, Biographies, Prefaces, Inscriptions, treatises on Medicine, Astrology, Astronomy, Metaphysical and moral questions were invariably written in metre, so that there was practically no purpose. The only branch of literature where we see the prose style much employed has been that of the learned commentaries on ancient works. Prose holds good in Tamil literature too. Tamil poetry has been in existence from the very beginning of the Christian era; wheras Tamil prose puts in its appearance only from the time of Constantius Beschi at least (1740 A.D.).
	The absence of paper and printing also accounts for the dearth of Prose writings. To write long prose works on palmyra leaves would be very tedious and it is no wonder that our forefathers did not think it worth while to waste their time in writing stories or tales in prose. The difficulty of the writing materials necessitated them to seek after compression of expression; and this they founal in poetry. This same difficulty accounts for the brevity and terseness of Commentaries on the poetical works. Adiyarkku Nallar, after writing an elaborate annota-tion on the first two lines of the Padhigam of Silappatikaram says, “Lest the Commentary should get too long, I refrin from annotating the whole book in this elaborate fashion.” If our ancient authors had the same Conveniences that we have now, surely, we would now be in possession of elaborate Commentaries and a good number of Prose works.
	My object in writing this work of fiction is to supply the want of prose works in Tamil a want which is admitted and lamented by all.
- S. Vedanayagam Pilli. Preface to the
first Edition, 1885.

4.	Rev. N. Samuel
5.	The Madras School Book Society.
